Structured Composition of Verifiable Atomic Insights for Table-to-Report Generation
用于表格到报告生成的可验证原子洞察的结构化组合
Table-to-report generation refers to the task of automatically generating article-level analyt- ical reports from relational tables and is an essential capability for automated data science and decision support. Its central challenge lies in systematically discovering verifiable com- posite insights across tables, attributes, and analytical perspectives, and organizing them into coherent, complete, and traceable evidence chains. Existing methods primarily rely on sequential, reactive data agents or direct Large Language Model(LLM) generation. They suffer from exploration bias: early local observations constrain subsequent actions, causing models to focus prematurely on local analyzes and miss cross-table or cross-dimensional evidence. We propose ComInsight, which reformulates insight discovery as the composition of atomic evidences. We first define an atomic insight as the smallest executable analytical unit conforming to a predefined analysis pattern and enumerate all valid atomic insights from database schema and content. These atoms are then organized into a multi-relational insight graph, where nodes represent verified data facts and edges encode logical, temporal, or hierarchical relations. Finally, a set of composition operators systematically fuses atomic nodes into higher-order composite conclusions. Every composite output is accompanied by executable SQL and fine-grained provenance, ensuring full verifiability. Across three benchmarks InsightBench, DDR-Bench, and T2R-Bench, ComInsight consistently outperforms strong baselines in factual correctness, novelty, and structural completeness. We believe ComInsight offers a reliable, efficient, and explainable path toward table-to-report generation.
中文翻译
表格到报告生成是指从关系型表格自动生成文章级分析报告，是自动化数据科学和决策支持的关键能力。其核心挑战在于系统性地发现跨表格、属性和分析视角的可验证复合洞察，并将其组织成连贯、完整且可追溯的证据链。现有方法主要依赖于顺序的、反应式的数据智能体或直接的大语言模型（LLM）生成，存在探索偏差：早期的局部观察会限制后续行为，导致模型过早关注局部分析，遗漏跨表或跨维度的证据。我们提出ComInsight，将洞察发现重新定义为原子证据的组合。首先，我们将原子洞察定义为符合预设分析模式的最小可执行分析单元，并从数据库模式和内容中枚举所有有效的原子洞察。这些原子被组织成一个多关系洞察图，其中节点代表已验证的数据事实，边编码逻辑、时间或层次关系。最后，一组组合算子系统地将原子节点融合为更高阶的复合结论。每个复合输出都附带可执行的SQL和细粒度溯源信息，确保完全可验证性。在三个基准测试InsightBench、DDR-Bench和T2R-Bench上，ComInsight在事实正确性、新颖性和结构完整性方面持续优于强基线。我们认为ComInsight为表格到报告生成提供了一条可靠、高效且可解释的路径。
👤 Teng Lin, Xinyu Liu, Nan Tang 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

CLIMB: Confidence-Guided Complementary Evidence for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation
CLIMB：置信度引导的互补证据用于多模态检索增强生成
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, but knowledge-intensive visual question answering often requires external textual evidence beyond the image and the model's parametric knowledge. Existing multimodal RAG systems commonly rely on Top-$K$ retrieval or reranking, which may return redundant passages and provide limited control over whether an answer update is sufficiently supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose \textit{CLIMB}, a training-free inference-time framework for multimodal RAG. CLIMB first constructs a compact complementary evidence pool using an MMR-style objective that balances query relevance and passage-level redundancy. It then performs confidence-controlled refinement within this fixed pool: an R/E/C critic scores passages by relevance, evidence specificity, and cross-modal alignment, while an evidence-grounded confidence estimator accepts an updated answer only when the estimated confidence increases. This design provides a simple stopping criterion and reduces unnecessary refinement without modifying the underlying retriever or MLLM. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that CLIMB consistently improves over retrieval-augmented multimodal baselines. Ablations further indicate that complementary pooling, critic-based scoring, and iterative confidence-controlled refinement each contribute to the final performance.
中文翻译
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）展现出强大的视觉推理能力，但知识密集型的视觉问答通常需要超出图像和模型参数知识的外部文本证据。现有的多模态RAG系统通常依赖Top-K检索或重排序，可能返回冗余段落，并且对答案更新是否充分由检索证据支持缺乏有效控制。我们提出CLIMB，一种无需训练的推理时多模态RAG框架。CLIMB首先使用MMR风格的目标构建一个紧凑的互补证据池，平衡查询相关性和段落层面的冗余性。然后在此固定池内进行置信度控制的精炼：一个R/E/C评鉴者根据相关性、证据特异性和跨模态对齐性评分段落，而一个基于证据的置信度估计器仅在估计置信度提升时才接受更新的答案。该设计提供了一个简单的停止标准，减少了不必要的精炼，且无需修改底层检索器或MLLM。在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek上的实验表明，CLIMB始终优于检索增强的多模态基线。消融实验进一步表明，互补池构建、评鉴者评分和迭代置信度控制精炼均对最终性能有贡献。
👤 Hang Gao, Wujiang Xu, Zhixing Zhang, Kai Mei, Jingyi Yang et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

SyntaxBench: A Statistical Diagnostic Framework for Character-Level Reasoning in Large Language Models
SyntaxBench：大语言模型字符级推理的统计诊断框架
Large language models are increasingly used where small syntactic errors matter, yet character-level reasoning is still evaluated mostly through isolated probes and aggregate accuracy. We introduce SyntaxBench, a diagnostic benchmark and statistical evaluation framework for character-level reasoning. It contains five core tasks, character counting, letter containment, palindrome detection, edit distance, and longest-string selection, plus index_to_span, a harder substring-extraction stress test. The five core tasks use paired English and character-length-matched random-string inputs. index_to_span documents share a 200-500 word band and are not character-length matched. All six tasks use zero-, one-, and four-shot prompts. We evaluate eight open-weight models from 2B to 32B parameters across 11 reasoning-mode configurations. The framework reports exact-match and relaxed accuracy, Cohen's kappa, paired McNemar tests with odds ratios, bootstrap confidence intervals, Kendall's tau, class-conditional metrics, tokenization analysis, and multiple-comparison-corrected tests. Three findings stand out. First, tokenization shapes accuracy: random strings are more character-visible than English strings (1.892 vs. 3.169 characters per token), and character-counting accuracy falls as English words occupy more tokens. Second, reasoning mode is not uniformly helpful: Gemma4-31B is nearly unchanged across modes on the near-saturated tasks, while Qwen3.6-27B is worse with thinking on palindrome detection (0.952 non-thinking vs. 0.886 thinking at four-shot). Third, index_to_span remains largely unsolved; the best four-shot exact-match accuracy is 6.75%. Character-level evaluation needs controlled inputs, paired tests, and analyses of tokenization and reasoning mode rather than aggregate accuracy alone.
中文翻译
大语言模型越来越多地应用于对微小语法错误敏感的场景，但字符级推理仍主要通过孤立探测和聚合准确率进行评估。我们引入SyntaxBench，一个用于字符级推理的诊断基准和统计评估框架。它包含五个核心任务：字符计数、字母包含、回文检测、编辑距离和最长字符串选择，以及一个更难的子串提取压力测试index_to_span。五个核心任务使用配对的英文和字符长度匹配的随机字符串输入。index_to_span文档共享200-500词范围，但不进行字符长度匹配。所有六个任务均使用零样本、一样本和四样本提示。我们在8个开放权重模型（2B至32B参数）上，跨11种推理模式配置进行评估。该框架报告精确匹配和宽松准确率、Cohen's kappa、配对McNemar检验与优势比、Bootstrap置信区间、Kendall's tau、类别条件指标、分词分析以及多重比较校正测试。三个发现尤为突出：第一，分词影响准确率：随机字符串比英文字符串更具字符可见性（每标记1.892 vs. 3.169个字符），且随着英文单词占据更多标记，字符计数准确率下降；第二，推理模式并非普遍有益：Gemma4-31B在接近饱和的任务上各模式间几乎无变化，而Qwen3.6-27B在回文检测任务上使用思考模式反而更差（四样本下非思考0.952 vs. 思考0.886）；第三，index_to_span仍基本未解决；最佳四样本精确匹配准确率为6.75%。字符级评估需要受控输入、配对测试以及对分词和推理模式的分析，而非仅依赖聚合准确率。
👤 Mohsen Larni, Sobhan Ebrahimi Azar, Pouyan Nahed, Kazem Taghva 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03215]

StanceEval 2026: The Second Stance Detection Shared Task
StanceEval 2026：第二届立场检测共享任务
StanceEval 2026 is the second edition of the StanceEval shared task series on stance detection in Arabic social media text. Stance detection aims to identify a writer's stance toward a given topic. Given a tweet and a target, participating systems must determine whether the writer's stance is Favor, Against, or None. This edition focuses on cross-target generalization across two distinct evaluation tracks: Track 1 evaluates thematically related cross-target transfer (testing on Women Driving, related to Women Empowerment from training data), while Track 2 evaluates cross-domain transfer to completely unseen targets (E-Cars and Trimester System). The shared task attracted 80 registered teams from 12 countries. During the evaluation phase, 30 unique teams submitted entries, with 21 teams officially ranked in Track 1 and 13 in Track 2 following validation filtering, and 20 teams submitting system-description papers. Participating teams employed diverse methodologies, including fine-tuned pretrained language models, prompt-based and retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs), fine-tuned LLMs, and hybrid cascades. Top systems achieved impressive $F_{avg2}$ scores of 0.8994 on Track 1 and 0.9400 on Track 2, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (0.7366 and 0.7475, respectively), where $F_{avg2}$ denotes the macro-averaged F1 score over the Favor and Against classes. Counterintuitively, performance on the unseen targets was higher than on the related target, a disparity could be driven by extreme target polarization, class imbalance, and dialectal or sarcastic nuance across topics.
中文翻译
StanceEval 2026是阿拉伯语社交媒体文本立场检测系列共享任务的第二届。立场检测旨在识别作者对某一话题的态度。给定一条推文和一个目标，参赛系统需判断作者立场是支持、反对还是无立场。本版聚焦于跨目标泛化，包含两个独立评估赛道：Track 1评估主题相关跨目标迁移（在训练数据中涉及女性驾驶，测试目标为女性赋权），Track 2评估跨领域迁移至完全未见过的目标（电动车型号和三 trimester 制度）。该共享任务吸引了来自12个国家的80支注册团队参与。评估阶段共有30支独立团队提交结果，经验证过滤后，21支团队在Track 1中正式排名，13支在Track 2中排名，另有20支团队提交系统描述论文。参赛团队采用了多种方法，包括微调预训练语言模型、基于提示和检索增强的大语言模型（LLMs）、微调LLMs以及混合级联模型。顶尖系统在Track 1上取得了0.8994的$F_{avg2}$得分，在Track 2上达到0.9400，远超最强基线（分别为0.7366和0.7475），其中$F_{avg2}$表示对支持和反对类别的宏平均F1得分。出人意料的是，对未见目标的表现反而高于对相关目标的表现，这种差异可能由目标极端极化、类别不平衡以及不同话题间的方言或讽刺细微差别驱动。
👤 Rasha Albalawi, Nuha Albadi, Hamzah Luqman, Asma Yamani, Maram Kurdi et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03195]

Source Preference in the Wild: How LLM Agents Favor Items by Source, and How to Reduce It
真实世界中的源偏好：LLM智能体如何偏好来源，以及如何减少它
As LLM agents decide on users' behalf which product to buy, which hotel to book, or which paper to cite, a preference for items from certain sources (the sites or services they come from) shapes what users receive and which sources are selected. We study source preference in end-to-end search with 12 agent models across three domains. Comparing items from different sources that satisfy the same requirements at the same position, we find that each model prefers some sources and avoids others in every domain, largely agreeing on which. This preference can outweigh how well items satisfy the request: an item satisfying one requirement fewer is selected about two-thirds of the time when it comes from a preferred source and the better one from a dispreferred source, but almost never in the reverse case. The information identifying an item's source affects selection by itself: hiding it weakens the preference, and relabeling an item with a preferred source raises its selection rate. We test two routes to this preference: training that rewards better items can make a source a shortcut for requirement satisfaction, and missing information can trigger preconceptions about the source. Supplying missing information or a prompt countering these preconceptions reduces source preference.
中文翻译
当LLM智能体代表用户决定购买何种产品、预订哪家酒店或引用哪篇论文时，对某些来源（即其所属网站或服务）的偏好会影响用户所获得的内容以及哪些来源被选中。我们研究了在端到端搜索中12种代理模型在三个领域中的源偏好现象。在满足相同需求且处于相同位置的不同来源项目之间进行比较，我们发现每种模型在每个领域都倾向于某些来源而避开其他来源，且共识度很高。这种偏好甚至可能超过项目满足请求的程度：当一个项目少满足一项需求但来自偏好来源时，约三分之二情况下仍会被选中，而来自非偏好来源的更好项目几乎不会被选中。仅标识项目来源的信息本身就会影响选择：隐藏来源信息会削弱偏好，而将项目重新标记为偏好来源则会提高其被选中的概率。我们测试了两种形成该偏好的路径：训练中奖励优质项目可能使某来源成为满足需求的捷径；缺失信息可能触发对来源的先入为主观念。提供缺失信息或使用反制这些先入观念的提示可以减少源偏好。
👤 Jonghyun Song, Haewon Park, Jeonghoon Shim, Woojung Song, Yohan Jo 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03190]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Not Until the Evidence Says So: Teaching LLM Investigators When to Close a Case
直到证据表明为止：教导大语言模型调查员何时结案
Accident, defect and outage investigations end with a decision that ordinary question answering never faces: whether the evidence gathered so far is enough to close the case. We study this decision for LLM investigators, which request evidence from a case file, revise their hypotheses, and either close the case with a conclusion grounded in what they read or leave it open and name what is missing. This judgment does not come with capability: an untrained 9B model overstates its evidence in 97% of its answers, and a frontier model that identifies the right cause in 84% of cases still overstates in 91% and closes 17 of the 41 cases whose official finding is "cause undetermined". Measuring it is also non-trivial: the source of a case largely predicts its label, and a rule that reads only the source reaches 83.0 balanced accuracy on our test cases. We therefore evaluate closure with three tests: closure accuracy, reported against this rule and within each source; evidence dependence, which removes the grounds of a conclusion and checks whether the model stops closing; and conclusion and gap quality, a judged checklist of what the model asserts and what it says is missing. We build Nautil, 731 audited cases from aviation, rail, maritime, chemical-safety and vehicle-defect reports and production server incidents, with teacher trajectories, an out-of-distribution test set and counterfactual evidence versions. Fine-tuning a 9B model on these trajectories makes its closures follow the evidence: removing the grounds lowers its closure rate by 26 points relative to a matched control, overstatement falls from 97% to 35%, and correct, non-overstated conclusions rise from 3% to 43%. Reinforcement learning that rewards only the closure decision then raises balanced accuracy from 69.2 to 83.3, on par with the teacher, and within-source accuracy from 60.4 to 74.1, at some cost in evidence dependence.
中文翻译
事故、缺陷和中断调查以一个普通问答从未面临过的决策结束：目前收集的证据是否足以结案。我们研究了大语言模型作为调查员在这一决策上的表现，它们从案件文件中请求证据，修改假设，并基于所读内容得出结论来结案，或保持开放并指出缺失的内容。这种判断并不与能力直接相关：未经训练的9B模型在其97%的回答中夸大了证据；即使前沿模型在84%的案例中正确识别原因，仍会在91%的情况下夸大，并在41个官方结论为“原因不明”的案件中结案17个。衡量这一能力也非易事：案件来源很大程度上可预测其标签，仅阅读来源的规则在我们的测试案例中达到83.0%的平衡准确率。因此，我们通过三项测试评估结案行为：结案准确率（相对于该规则及各来源内比较）、证据依赖性（移除结论依据后检查模型是否停止结案），以及结论与缺失项质量（由人工评判的清单，评估模型所断言内容及其指出的缺失部分）。我们构建了Nautil数据集，包含来自航空、铁路、海事、化学品安全和车辆缺陷报告及生产服务器事件的731个经审核案件，包含教师轨迹、分布外测试集和反事实证据版本。对9B模型在这些轨迹上进行微调后，其结案行为更符合证据：移除依据后结案率下降26个百分点，相比对照组，夸大现象从97%降至35%，正确且不夸大的结论从3%提升至43%。随后采用仅奖励结案决策的强化学习，使平衡准确率从69.2提升至83.3，达到教师水平；来源内准确率从60.4提升至74.1，但代价是证据依赖性略有下降。
👤 Tingzhu Bi, Ping Wang, Meng Ma 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03136]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Investigating the Role of Reasoning-Language Alignment in Monolingual Retrieval-Augmented Generation
探究单语检索增强生成中推理-语言对齐的作用
Reasoning traces improve large language models (LLMs), but current models are trained to reason mostly in English. It has been shown that forcing a model to reason in another language degrades accuracy, even when the reasoning language matches the language of the prompt -- but only for a setting where the model reasons over a short prompt. Here, we ask whether the same holds for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where the model must read and integrate a large amount of retrieved evidence in the target language. To study this, we build a fully monolingual German RAG question-answering testbed over the fictional world of the tabletop role-playing game The Dark Eye, a domain that is richly documented in German but too niche for the model to answer from memory, so that it has to rely on retrieval. Varying the forced reasoning language of an agentic RAG system on this testbed, we find that aligning the reasoning language with the language of the query and the retrieved documents helps. Forced German reasoning outperforms forced French, although the model benchmarks higher in French, so the benefit comes from alignment and not from language proficiency. The advantage grows when the retrieved context is richer and structure-aware. However, forced German only reaches the level of the model's native, unconstrained English reasoning without surpassing it, showing that native multilingual reasoning is needed. We publicly release the testbed and QA benchmark.
中文翻译
推理链能够提升大语言模型的表现，但当前模型主要在英语中进行训练。已有研究表明，强制模型在另一种语言中推理会降低准确率，即使推理语言与提示语言一致——但仅适用于模型仅处理简短提示的情形。本文探讨这一现象是否同样存在于检索增强生成（RAG）场景中，即模型需阅读并整合大量目标语言的检索证据。为此，我们在虚构桌游《暗眼》的世界构建了一个全单语德语RAG问答测试平台，该领域在德语中有丰富文档记录，但过于小众，模型无法凭记忆回答，必须依赖检索。在该测试平台上，我们改变代理型RAG系统强制推理语言，发现将推理语言与查询和检索文档的语言对齐有助于性能提升。强制德语推理优于强制法语，尽管模型在法语上的基准表现更高，说明优势来自语言对齐而非语言熟练度。当检索上下文更丰富且具备结构意识时，该优势更加明显。然而，强制德语推理仅达到模型原生无约束英语推理的水平，未能超越，表明原生多语言推理仍是必需的。我们已公开发布该测试平台和问答基准。
👤 Oliver Hauck, Mario Sanz-Guerrero, Katharina von der Wense 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03112]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Building Interpretable Feature Representations for Resume-Vacancy Matching by Distilling Production LLM Signals
通过蒸馏生产级大语言模型信号构建可解释的简历-职位匹配特征表示
Matching candidates to vacancies is central to recruitment, and a recruiter needs to see why a candidate fits, not only a single opaque relevance score. We provide this evidence as named, interpretable matching dimensions recruiters can act on - eight in our current deployment. We propose a two-part approach. The first is an LLM-based labeler whose prompts and feature definitions were refined from recruiter feedback while it served as an earlier production matching stage. In the current architecture, it is used only for offline labeling and is not called on online requests. The second is a feature bi-encoder distilled from it: a LoRA-adapted embedding backbone with compact per-dimension heads that runs on CPU and serves all online requests. Both parts keep improving: prompts are revised as feedback arrives, and the bi-encoder is retrained on the updated labels. The model is trained on 168,772 labeled vacancy-resume pairs (17,921 vacancies and 180,030 resumes). Recruiters using the service can confirm or revise surfaced feature predictions. On 927 recruiter-recorded values from this selected production-feedback subset, the deployed student agrees with the recorded decisions in 888 cases (95.79%). This is operational, non-blinded agreement rather than an independent human evaluation.
中文翻译
将候选人与职位匹配是招聘的核心，而招聘人员不仅需要一个模糊的相关性评分，更需要理解为何某位候选人合适。我们提供可命名、可解释的匹配维度作为证据，招聘人员可据此采取行动——当前部署中包含八个维度。我们提出两阶段方法：第一阶段是基于大语言模型的标注器，其提示和特征定义根据招聘人员反馈不断优化，曾作为早期生产匹配阶段使用；在当前架构中，它仅用于离线标注，不参与在线请求。第二阶段是一个从该标注器蒸馏而来的特征双编码器：一个LoRA适配的嵌入主干网络，搭配紧凑的每维头，可在CPU上运行并服务所有在线请求。两部分持续改进：提示随反馈更新，双编码器也基于新标注数据重新训练。模型在168,772个标注的职位-简历对上训练（共17,921个职位和180,030份简历）。使用该服务的招聘人员可确认或修正浮现的特征预测。在从选定生产反馈子集中记录的927个值中，部署的学生模型与记录决策一致达888例（95.79%）。这是实际运行中的非盲态一致性，而非独立的人工评估。

Ask, Relax, or Act? Evaluating Actionable Indeterminacy in LLM Preference Reasoning
问、放松还是行动？评估大语言模型偏好推理中的可行动不确定性
An LLM agent can recognize uncertainty yet still choose the wrong next step: asking when action is already justified, or seeking clarification when the constraints must change. We formalize actionable indeterminacy: act when an accepted action is shared across all admissible preferences or objectives, clarify when each possibility is feasible but no action is shared, and propose a minimum-cost permitted constraint repair when the request is infeasible. We construct a solver-grounded benchmark spanning object allocation, meeting scheduling, apartment choice, and stable matching. Matched pairs retain the same source while changing whether intervention is necessary, and evaluation separates decision correctness, matched-pair reliability, and fully correct responses. Our findings reveal a recurring difficulty in recognizing when intervention is unnecessary: models can identify situations requiring clarification or repair yet still intervene when a justified action already exists. Correct decision labels also fail to guarantee usable actions, questions, or repairs. Crucially, response requirements shape not only how decisions are expressed but also which decisions are made. Making the required content explicit substantially improves fully correct responses and can change intervention decisions, even when outputs are already parseable. These findings highlight that reliable agency requires more than recognizing uncertainty: it requires intervening only when necessary and translating the chosen next step into a verifiable response.
中文翻译
大语言模型代理能够识别不确定性，但仍可能做出错误的下一步选择：在行动已合理时提问，或在约束必须改变时寻求澄清。我们形式化了可行动不确定性：当所有可接受偏好或目标共享同一行动时采取行动；当每种可能性都可行但无共同行动时进行澄清；当请求不可行时提出最小成本允许的约束修复。我们构建了一个基于求解器的基准测试，涵盖物品分配、会议安排、公寓选择和稳定匹配。配对样本保留相同来源，但改变是否需要干预，评估分离了决策正确性、配对可靠性及完全正确响应。研究发现，模型反复难以识别何时无需干预：即使能识别需澄清或修复的情境，仍会在已有合理行动时进行干预。正确的决策标签也无法保证产生可用的动作、问题或修复。关键的是，响应要求不仅影响决策表达方式，也影响决策本身。明确所需内容显著提升了完全正确响应的比例，甚至可改变干预决策，即便输出已可解析。这些发现表明，可靠代理不仅需要识别不确定性，还需仅在必要时干预，并将所选下一步转化为可验证的响应。

Recursive Self-Improvement in Unified Multimodal Models
统一多模态模型中的递归自我改进
Unified multimodal models (UMMs) understand and generate both text and images, which lets a model produce its own training data. Existing self-improvement in UMMs keeps supervision on the visual side, where image understanding judges image generation. We propose recursive cross-capability self-improvement (RSI), a training loop in which the text and visual abilities of a UMM supply training data for one another. In each round, the model generates images and reads them to find where it falls short. It then writes programs aimed at these shortcomings, and execution verifies every result against its specification. Verified renders train image generation, while labeled renders and the model's own correct programs train visual understanding and program writing. Program execution thus acts as a source of truth outside the model, so errors do not accumulate across rounds. We study RSI on charts and build BasicChartBench to evaluate open models early in training. On requests worded differently from training, four rounds of RSI raise the score from 45.7% to 60.2%, while continued training stays at 46.3%. Verified construction carries most of the gain, and targeting the model's failures adds 3.5%. Along the way, the share of verified programs rises from 48.9% to 95.2%, and the reader's accuracy on edited renders rises from 55.6% to 87.4%.
中文翻译
统一多模态模型（UMMs）能够理解并生成文本与图像，使模型可自主生成训练数据。现有UMM的自我改进仍依赖于视觉侧的监督，即由图像理解来评判图像生成。我们提出递归跨能力自我改进（RSI），一种训练循环，其中文本与视觉能力相互提供训练数据。每轮中，模型生成图像并阅读以识别自身不足；随后编写针对这些缺陷的程序，执行后根据规范验证每个结果。经验证的渲染图像用于训练图像生成，而标注的渲染图像及模型自身的正确程序则用于训练视觉理解和程序编写。程序执行作为模型外部的真理来源，避免错误在多轮中累积。我们在图表任务上研究RSI，并构建BasicChartBench以评估训练早期的开放模型。在与训练语料表述不同的请求上，四轮RSI使得分从45.7%提升至60.2%，而持续训练仅维持在46.3%。验证性构建贡献了大部分提升，针对性修复失败项额外带来3.5%增益。过程中，验证程序占比从48.9%升至95.2%，读者对编辑后图像的准确率从55.6%升至87.4%。

OmniConfess: Eliciting Token Confessions to Mitigate Omni-Modal Hallucination
OmniConfess：通过提取令牌供词缓解全模态幻觉
Omni-modal large language models (OmniLLMs) unify text, images, audio, and video, yet hallucinate when generation relies on the wrong evidence. Existing inference-time methods can reduce hallucinations, but rarely reveal which evidence sustains a generated commitment. We introduce OmniConfess, a training-free method for mitigating omni-modal hallucinations. It fixes a candidate response and re-scores it at token resolution under controlled channel-wise evidence interventions, producing a structured token-by-channel confession that reveals the response's evidential dependence. OmniConfess uses this confession to preserve grounded content and correct commitments driven by irrelevant or contradictory evidence. To evaluate OmniConfess, we construct OmniHalluBench, a 3,540-example benchmark built from six datasets spanning text, image, audio, and video settings and both judgment and free-form generation. Experiments show that OmniConfess mitigates hallucinations across heterogeneous modality and task settings. Our code and benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/RongHuiQiang/OmniConfess.
中文翻译
全模态大型语言模型（OmniLLMs）整合文本、图像、音频和视频，但在生成依赖错误证据时会产生幻觉。现有推理阶段方法虽可减少幻觉，却很少揭示哪些证据支撑了生成内容。我们提出OmniConfess，一种无需训练的全模态幻觉缓解方法。它固定候选回答，在受控的通道级证据干预下以令牌粒度重新评分，生成结构化的逐令牌-通道供词，揭示回答对证据的依赖关系。OmniConfess利用此供词保留有依据的内容，并修正由无关或矛盾证据驱动的错误承诺。为评估OmniConfess，我们构建了OmniHalluBench，一个包含3,540个样本的基准，涵盖文本、图像、音频和视频设置，以及判断与自由生成任务。实验表明，OmniConfess在异构模态与任务设置下均能有效缓解幻觉。代码与基准已公开于https://github.com/RongHuiQiang/OmniConfess。

OLMo-Detect: A Multi-Stage, Confounder-Controlled Benchmark for Membership Inference on Large Language Models
OLMo-Detect：一种多阶段、混淆控制的大型语言模型成员推理基准测试
Membership inference on large language models (LLMs) aims to determine whether a given text sample was included in an LLM's training data, without access to its training corpus. Despite recent progress, existing benchmarks suffer from three limitations: limited coverage of training stages, insufficient distributional alignment between members and non-members, and lack of rigorous filtering of non-members against the training corpus. To address these limitations, we propose OLMo-Detect, a multi-stage, confounder-controlled benchmark built upon the fully open OLMo 2 pipeline. OLMo-Detect spans pre-training, mid-training, and post-training, explicitly aligns members and non-members on three key axes, and rigorously filters non-members via infini-gram. To assess robustness to distribution shifts, we further introduce OLMo-Detect (Shifted), a variant where members are misaligned with non-members. We evaluate 15 unsupervised and 3 supervised membership inference attacks (MIAs) across the OLMo 2 family, finding that: (i) overall performance is limited: the best unsupervised and supervised MIAs both reach an AUC of only 0.68, and supervised MIAs degrade under cross-domain evaluation; (ii) MIA performance peaks at mid-training and is lower at pre-training and post-training, a pattern driven by data type rather than a stage effect: curated math data is far more detectable than other types; (iii) overall scores improve from 1B to 13B but plateau at 32B; and (iv) no unsupervised MIA is robust to distribution shifts, with AUCs shifting by up to 0.42. Finally, we find that our findings on OLMo 2 generalize to OLMo 3 and non-OLMo models.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）的成员推理旨在在无法访问训练语料库的情况下判断给定文本样本是否包含在LLM的训练数据中。尽管近期取得进展，现有基准仍存在三个局限：训练阶段覆盖有限、成员与非成员之间的分布对齐不足，以及对非成员缺乏严格的训练语料库过滤。为解决这些问题，我们提出了OLMo-Detect，一个基于完全开源OLMo 2流程的多阶段、混淆控制基准测试。OLMo-Detect涵盖预训练、中期训练和后训练阶段，明确在三个关键维度上对齐成员与非成员，并通过infini-gram严格过滤非成员。为评估对分布偏移的鲁棒性，我们进一步引入OLMo-Detect（Shifted），其中成员与非成员存在错位。我们在OLMo 2系列模型上评估了15种无监督和3种有监督的成员推理攻击（MIAs），发现：(i) 总体性能有限：最佳无监督和有监督MIAs的AUC均仅为0.68，且有监督MIAs在跨领域评估中表现下降；(ii) MIA性能在中期训练时达到峰值，预训练和后训练阶段较低，这一模式由数据类型驱动而非阶段效应：经过筛选的数学数据远比其他类型更易检测；(iii) 总体得分从1B到13B提升，但在32B时趋于饱和；(iv) 无任何无监督MIA对分布偏移具有鲁棒性，AUC变化高达0.42。最后，我们发现OLMo 2上的发现可推广至OLMo 3及非OLMo模型。

Output Language Confusion under Multilingual Prompt Contamination
多语言提示污染下的输出语言混淆
Standard factual benchmarks assume clean monolingual prompts and exact-match scoring, two assumptions that break simultaneously in real-world multilingual deployment, from retrieval-augmented generation pipelines returning mixed-language passages to users pasting multilingual web content. We introduce Multilingual Distractor Interference (MDI), a lightweight and fully replicable evaluation protocol requiring no new data or annotation, in which factual questions are preceded by a semantically irrelevant foreign-language sentence, and evaluate five instruction-tuned LLMs across TruthfulQA and TriviaQA under eight distractor conditions (40,000 evaluations). Our central finding is a metric confound: for Llama-3.1-8B under a Hindi distractor, 58% of responses switch to Devanagari script, yielding a raw hallucination proxy of 0.710, but manual review reveals that 120 of 148 script-switched responses that were correct under clean conditions remain semantically correct despite being written in the wrong script, reducing the adjusted semantic hallucination rate to 0.470. All other models respond through abstention escalation with no hallucination increase. A paragraph-length English distractor triggers near-universal abstention (0.806-0.998) across all models, consistent with reading-comprehension confusion, a failure mode with direct consequences for multilingual RAG pipelines. TruthfulQA multiple-choice accuracy is unaffected under all single-sentence conditions. These results show that exact-match hallucination rates in mixed-language settings should be decomposed into script-switching and semantic error components before drawing conclusions about model reliability.
中文翻译
标准的事实性基准测试假设提示为纯单语且采用精确匹配评分，这两个假设在真实世界的多语言部署中会同时失效，例如检索增强生成管道返回混合语言段落，或用户粘贴多语言网页内容。我们引入多语言干扰干扰（MDI），一种轻量级且完全可复现的评估协议，无需新数据或标注，在事实性问题前添加一句语义无关的外语句子，并在TruthfulQA和TriviaQA上评估五种指令微调的LLM，共八种干扰条件（40,000次评估）。核心发现是指标混淆：对于Llama-3.1-8B在印地语干扰下，58%的响应切换到天城文脚本，原始幻觉代理值为0.710，但人工审查显示，在清洁条件下正确的148个脚本切换响应中有120个在语义上仍然正确，因此调整后的语义幻觉率降至0.470。所有其他模型通过拒绝上升应对，未出现幻觉增加。一段英文干扰文本引发几乎所有模型的普遍拒绝（0.806–0.998），与阅读理解混淆一致，这种失败模式对多语言RAG管道有直接后果。TruthfulQA多项选择准确率在所有单句条件下均不受影响。这些结果表明，在混合语言环境中，精确匹配的幻觉率应分解为脚本切换和语义错误两部分，才能得出关于模型可靠性的合理结论。

How Robust Is Multimodal Claim Verification to LLM Rewriting?
大语言模型重写对多模态声明验证的鲁棒性如何？
LLMs are known to introduce stylistic changes into generated text, yet how these stylistic shifts affect model decisions on scientific tasks remains underexplored. In this paper, we focus on multimodal claim verification, where the goal is to determine whether a textual claim is grounded in a given piece of evidence. We apply two rewriting strategies: natural rewriting, which simulates how researchers routinely use LLMs to polish academic text, and controlled injection, which inserts a single LLM-associated word to isolate the effect of vocabulary choice. We evaluate 11 open-weight models spanning five VLM families and ranging from 2B to 38B parameters. We find that models are robust to these modifications: most show no significant drop in accuracy, and compared to prior work on review-score manipulation, verification appears far more stable. However, consistent probability shifts do occur. Hedging-oriented conditions produce significant shifts across nearly all models, while boosting conditions show a weaker effect and general polishing conditions (e.g., grammar correction, fluency improvement) have little effect.

大语言模型（LLM）已知会改变生成文本的风格，但这些风格变化如何影响模型在科学任务中的决策仍缺乏深入研究。本文聚焦于多模态声明验证，目标是判断一个文本声明是否基于给定证据成立。我们采用两种重写策略：自然重写，模拟研究人员常用LLM润色学术文本的常规做法；受控注入，插入一个与LLM相关的词汇以隔离词汇选择的影响。我们评估了11个开放权重模型，涵盖五个视觉-语言模型（VLM）家族，参数量从2B到38B不等。结果发现，模型对这些修改具有较强的鲁棒性：大多数模型准确率未显著下降，相较于以往关于评分操纵的研究，验证任务表现出更高的稳定性。然而，概率分布的一致性偏移确实存在：以缓和为导向的条件在几乎所有模型中都引发显著偏移，而增强型条件影响较弱，一般润色条件（如语法修正、流畅性提升）则影响甚微。

Automatic Evaluation of Mental Health Stigma in Online Communication
在线交流中心理健康污名的自动评估
Mental health stigma has profoundly harmful impacts but its complexity makes it difficult to evaluate. Stigma may involve explicit derogation, but also subtler forms of blame, fear, paternalistic pity, social distancing, structural exclusion, and discrimination. We introduce a theory-grounded benchmark for automatic evaluation of mental health stigma in online communication, consisting of naturally occurring online news and social media text annotated with a fine-grained taxonomy of stigma across multiple mental health conditions. Our annotation framework comprises a binary stigma-detection task and a multi-level taxonomy covering (i) stigma mode, (ii) domain, and (iii) specific components of certain forms of stigma. We apply this framework to texts mentioning six mental health conditions and evaluate large language models alongside stigma-related classifiers for detecting sentiment, toxicity, and hate speech. Results show that mental health stigma is not well captured by models trained to detect these neighboring constructs, and that LLMs often overpredict stigma unless given explicit operational rules - mirroring the importance of decision rules in human annotation. We release the publicly available part of benchmark, annotations, prototypical exemplars of stigma and code at: https://github.com/jemimakang/mh_stigma.

心理健康污名具有深远的负面影响，但其复杂性使其难以评估。污名可能表现为明确贬低，也包括更隐蔽的形式，如责备、恐惧、居高临下的同情、社会疏离、结构性排斥及歧视。本文提出一个基于理论的基准，用于自动评估在线交流中的心理健康污名，包含自然发生的新闻与社交媒体文本，并针对多种精神健康状况标注了细粒度的污名分类体系。我们的标注框架包含二元污名检测任务和一个多层级分类体系，涵盖（i）污名形式、（ii）领域、（iii）特定污名形式的具体组成部分。我们将该框架应用于涉及六种精神健康状况的文本，并评估大型语言模型以及相关分类器在检测情感、毒性与仇恨言论方面的表现。结果表明，仅训练于邻近概念（如情感、毒性）的模型无法有效捕捉心理健康污名，且LLMs在未提供明确操作规则的情况下往往过度预测污名——这与人类标注中决策规则的重要性相呼应。我们公开发布了该基准的部分内容、标注数据、污名原型示例及代码：https://github.com/jemimakang/mh_stigma。
👤 Naomi Baes, Jemima Kang, Nick Haslam, Chris Groot, Alsa Wu et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

AptMQL-Bench: From Text-to-SQL to Text-to-MQL via Access-Pattern Schema Design and Data-Preserving Migration
AptMQL-Bench：通过访问模式架构设计与数据保持迁移，从文本到MQL
Document databases such as MongoDB are core infrastructure for modern applications, and natural-language interfaces to them---text-to-MQL---would let non-experts query complex, semi-structured data without mastering the query language. Progress on this task depends on high-quality benchmarks, which are most practically obtained by converting an existing text-to-SQL benchmark to the document setting. Unfortunately, existing efforts rely on heuristics for mechanical conversion: the document schema mirrors the relational foreign-key graph, and each query mirrors its source SQL. As a result in our experiments, these approaches fail to migrate 6 of 21 BIRD databases outright, silently drop up to 25.9\% of rows on others, and yield schemas whose ground-truth queries run over an order of magnitude slower as the data scales. We instead propose a conversion pipeline, driven by coding agents with human-in-the-loop verification, that designs each document schema from expected access patterns and rewrites queries to be MongoDB-native. Applying it to BIRD, we build an access-pattern-based text-to-MQL benchmark (AptMQL-Bench). It includes 21 document-oriented databases, 3,186 natural-language requests, and their associated MQL queries---whose databases are migrated from SQLite without data loss and scale efficiently. The strongest model, Claude Opus 4.5, achieves only 57.38\% accuracy without external knowledge evidence and 70.34\% with it. This indicates that realistic text-to-MQL generation remains challenging.
中文翻译
文档数据库（如MongoDB）是现代应用的核心基础设施，而自然语言接口——文本到MQL——能让非专家在不掌握查询语言的情况下查询复杂、半结构化数据。该任务的进步依赖于高质量的基准测试，最实际的方法是将现有的文本到SQL基准转换为文档场景。然而，现有方法依赖启发式机械转换：文档模式镜像关系型外键图，每个查询也镜像其源SQL。结果在我们的实验中，这些方法无法迁移21个BIRD数据库中的6个，无声地丢弃其他数据库高达25.9%的数据行，并导致随着数据规模扩大，真实查询运行速度慢一个数量级。我们提出一种由编码代理驱动、人工验证的转换流程，根据预期访问模式设计每个文档模式，并重写查询使其符合MongoDB原生特性。将其应用于BIRD，我们构建了一个基于访问模式的文本到MQL基准（AptMQL-Bench），包含21个面向文档的数据库、3,186条自然语言请求及其对应的MQL查询——数据库均从SQLite迁移而来，无数据丢失且可高效扩展。最强模型Claude Opus 4.5在无外部知识证据时仅达到57.38%准确率，在有外部知识时提升至70.34%。这表明现实中的文本到MQL生成仍具挑战性。

EpiWorld: Grounding LLM Policy Agents in Epidemiological World Models
EpiWorld：将大语言模型策略代理嵌入流行病学世界模型
Epidemic intervention policies are textual artefacts that human decision-makers interpret, justify, and revise through natural language, making large language models a natural candidate for epidemic policy reasoning. A naive LLM, however, lacks the epidemic dynamics needed to project intervention consequences, the quantitative surveillance signals required to assess severity, and the institutional constraints that define admissible actions. We present EpiWorld, a closed-loop framework that grounds an LLM policy actor in a learned action-conditioned epidemiological world model and a tiered skill library of public-health protocols, surveillance tools, and adaptive lessons accumulated through after-action analysis. Given a candidate intervention, the world model predicts regional epidemic evolution and enables fast counterfactual rollouts that provide feedback for policy selection and refinement. Outcomes of simulated futures are distilled into reusable lessons while protocol constraints remain fixed, allowing the decision process to improve without sacrificing interpretability or controllability. We evaluate both the world model and the end-to-end framework on retrospective COVID-19 and Influenza datasets: the world model achieves the best out-of-distribution Peak-MAE among all forecasting baselines, and the closed-loop framework reduces cumulative hospitalisation by up to 59% across datasets and by an average of ~16% across six LLM backbones, outperforming reinforcement-learning and optimal-control policy baselines.
中文翻译
流行病干预政策是人类决策者通过自然语言解释、辩护和修订的文本产物，使大型语言模型成为流行病政策推理的天然候选。然而，朴素的LLM缺乏预测干预后果所需的流行病动态、评估严重程度所需的定量监测信号以及定义可接受行动的制度约束。我们提出EpiWorld，一个闭环框架，将LLM策略代理嵌入一个学习得到的动作条件流行病学世界模型，以及一个分层技能库（包含公共卫生协议、监测工具和通过事后分析积累的适应性经验）。给定一个候选干预措施，世界模型可预测区域流行病演变，并支持快速反事实推演，为政策选择与优化提供反馈。模拟未来的结果被提炼为可复用的经验，而协议约束保持不变，使决策过程得以改进而不牺牲可解释性或可控性。我们在回顾性的COVID-19和流感数据集上评估了世界模型及端到端框架：世界模型在所有预测基线中实现了最佳的分布外峰值MAE；闭环框架在多个数据集上累计住院人数减少高达59%，平均减少约16%，优于强化学习和最优控制政策基线。

Beyond Correctness: Resolving Underspecification in Agentic Text-to-SQL
超越正确性：解决智能体文本到SQL中的未明确性问题
Agentic Text-to-SQL systems can interact with users to clarify underspecified queries before generating SQL. However, a correct execution result does not necessarily imply that the agent has adequately resolved the underlying underspecification: the agent may silently make unverified assumptions that happen to match the intended answer. We show that this behavior is driven in part by premature clarification termination. Although forcing an agent to ask more questions improves execution accuracy, ambiguities are concentrated in earlier interactions, making brute-force questioning inefficient. More importantly, even when explicitly prompted to plan its clarification process, the agent frequently abandons questions that it has already identified as relevant. To address this failure mode, we introduce PlanPool, which externalizes the clarification plan as a mutable question pool. Every planned question must be explicitly asked or dropped before submission, while newly discovered ambiguities can be added during interaction. Across three benchmarks derived from BIRD-Interact and Spider, PlanPool consistently improves ambiguity coverage and reduces silent failures over unconstrained and prompt-based alternatives, while maintaining competitive execution accuracy. Our results highlight an important distinction in agentic reasoning: identifying missing information is not sufficient, and the agent must also reliably maintain and resolve it before committing to an answer.
中文翻译
智能体文本到SQL系统可在生成SQL前与用户交互以澄清未明确查询。然而，执行结果正确并不意味着代理已充分解决底层未明确性：代理可能静默地做出未经验证的假设，恰好匹配预期答案。我们发现，这种行为部分源于过早终止澄清过程。虽然强制代理提问更多问题能提高执行准确率，但模糊性集中在早期交互阶段，使得盲目提问效率低下。更重要的是，即使明确提示代理规划澄清过程，它仍经常放弃已识别出的相关问题。为解决这一失败模式，我们引入PlanPool，将澄清计划外部化为一个可变的问题池。每个计划问题必须显式提出或放弃后才能提交，同时交互过程中新发现的模糊性也可添加。在基于BIRD-Interact和Spider衍生的三个基准上，PlanPool持续提升了模糊性覆盖范围并减少了沉默失败，同时保持与无约束和提示基方法相当的执行准确率。我们的结果突显了智能体推理中的一个重要区别：识别缺失信息并不足够，代理还必须可靠地维持并解决这些信息后才能提交答案。

TPBench: A Turning-Point Benchmark for Dialogue Compression
TPBench：对话压缩的转折点基准
A compressor can keep the facts of a dialogue and still drop the turn that changed them. A user corrects a price, reverses a choice, or adds a constraint. We call this failure turning-point eviction. One overall retention score hides it, because that score mixes what the user first wanted with what the user wants now. We introduce TPBench, which evaluates three complementary information targets at shared nominal retention budgets. P1 asks for the user's initial goal. P2 asks for the current value of a slot the user revised. P3 asks for both, in dialogues with a late annotated slot update. The current-value answers come from the human dialogue-state annotations of MultiWOZ and SGD. The initial-goal answer is the first sentence of the first user turn. Neither requires new crowdsourcing. The probe-specific evaluations rank compression methods differently. On the joint probe at a retained fraction of 0.30, every tested compressed method remains below full context with the main Llama reader. Deleting the turn that carries the update sharply lowers current-value accuracy, while deleting one matched irrelevant turn leaves it unchanged. A Mistral reader repeats the P2/P3 rankings and the joint-probe gap. Current-value recovery is tested on an additional corpus, LongMemEval-KU, and on Chinese RiSAWOZ: full context has the highest accuracy, and recency has the highest compressed-method mean in both evaluations.
中文翻译
压缩器可以保留对话的事实，但仍可能删除改变这些事实的回合。例如用户更正价格、撤销选择或增加约束。我们将这种失败称为‘转折点驱逐’。单一的整体保留分数会掩盖这一问题，因为该分数混合了用户最初的需求与当前需求。我们引入TPBench，评估在相同名义保留预算下的三个互补信息目标。P1要求获取用户的初始目标；P2要求获取用户修改过的槽位的当前值；P3要求同时获取两者，适用于后期标注槽位更新的对话。当前值答案来自MultiWOZ和SGD的人工对话状态标注，初始目标答案为首个用户回合的第一句话。两者均无需新的众包。探针特定评估对压缩方法排名不同。在保留比例为0.30的联合探针上，所有测试的压缩方法均低于完整上下文，主Llama阅读器表现最优。删除携带更新的回合会显著降低当前值准确率，而删除一个匹配的无关回合则无影响。Mistral阅读器重复了P2/P3的排名和联合探针差距。当前值恢复在额外语料LongMemEval-KU和中文RiSAWOZ上进行测试：完整上下文准确率最高，而在两种评估中，时效性方法的压缩方法平均得分最高。

Silent Dissent: LLM Agents That Yield to the Majority Still Represent Their Original Premise
沉默的异议：即使顺从多数意见，LLM代理仍保留其原始前提
Multi-agent debate is increasingly used to reach consensus among LLM agents, yet agents often yield to a unanimous majority. When an agent changes its answer, has it changed its mind or only its statement? We study this with two-hop factual questions whose intermediate entity (the bridge, e.g. the country in "the capital of the country where the Sagrada Familia is located") is never stated by anyone. Scripted peers, in the role of Asch's confederates, unanimously assert a wrong answer taken from another fact with a different bridge. At the moment the agent answers, we read the bridge from its residual stream with the Jacobian lens (J-lens) and, for comparison, the logit lens. In pre-registered tests on held-out facts with four open-weight models, agents of Qwen3.5-4B, Qwen3.6-27B and Gemma-4-E4B-it that gave in still represented their original bridge in the pre-registered layers below the output (hit@100 above a control entity: 0.85, 0.22 and 0.24), where the logit lens rarely ranked it among the top 100 tokens (0.00-0.06). These agents also represented the bridge behind the peers' answer, beyond a mention baseline. A pre-registered addendum hid the agent's earlier answer or removed it: agents that gave in still represented their original bridge in all four models (0.43, 0.29, 0.37 and 0.25 with the answer hidden), including Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, which barely did so with its answer in view (0.03). The premise can thus be computed from the question alone while the agent states the majority's answer. Hiding the earlier answer also changed conformity: Qwen3.5-4B gave in on 89% of questions instead of 8%. In exploratory interventions, injecting the bridge's J-lens direction brought agents back to their original answer only in the two Qwen models. Stated consensus in multi-agent debate can thus overstate agreement. We also report the negative results of our pre-registered program.
中文翻译
多代理辩论被越来越多地用于达成LLM代理之间的共识，但代理常常屈从于一致多数。当一个代理更改答案时，是真正改变了想法，还是仅改变了表述？我们通过两跳事实类问题研究这一问题，其中中间实体（桥接项，如“圣家堂所在国家的首都”中的国家）从未被任何人明确陈述。脚本化的同伴扮演Asch实验中的同谋者，一致声称一个来自另一事实且具有不同桥接项的错误答案。在代理作答的瞬间，我们使用雅可比透镜（J-lens）从残差流中读取桥接项，并与逻辑透镜进行对比。在四个开源权重模型上对预留事实进行预注册测试，Qwen3.5-4B、Qwen3.6-27B和Gemma-4-E4B-it等模型虽顺从多数，但在输出层以下的预注册层中仍能表示其原始桥接项（命中率@100高于控制实体：0.85、0.22和0.24），而逻辑透镜很少将其排进前100个词元（0.00–0.06）。这些代理还表示了同伴答案背后的桥接项，超越了提及基线。预注册补充实验隐藏或移除代理早期答案后，所有四个模型仍保留其原始桥接项表示（隐藏答案时命中率分别为0.43、0.29、0.37和0.25），包括Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，其在可见答案时几乎无法做到（命中率0.03）。因此，前提可仅从问题中计算得出，而代理则陈述多数意见。隐藏早期答案也改变了从众程度：Qwen3.5-4B从8%变为89%。探索性干预显示，仅在两个Qwen模型中注入桥接项的J-lens方向才能使代理回归原答案。因此，多代理辩论中的表面共识可能夸大了实际一致性。我们还报告了预注册研究计划的负面结果。
👤 Ziang Ni, Peng Zou 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Generalization of Large Language Models in Temporal Extraction Tasks
评估大语言模型在时间提取任务中的多维度泛化能力
Time and event expression extraction are fundamental temporal reasoning tasks, but the problem remains difficult due to annotation ambiguity, domain sensitivity, and unstable model behavior. Existing evaluations focus on in-domain performance, offering limited insight into reliability under distribution shifts. We evaluate multiple model configurations across families, architectures, and reasoning strategies over four dimensions of generalization, examining transfer from base performance, cross-dimensional correlations, and the effects of scale, architecture, and prompting. This provides a systematic study of how prompted LLMs generalize in time and event expression extraction tasks. We find that strong base-task performance generally predicts better generalization. However, this relationship weakens under substantial distribution shifts. Inductive prompting performs most consistently across domain shift, adversarial perturbations, compositionality, and length increase, while gains from scale, architecture, and deductive and abductive prompting strategies are uneven and dimension-specific. We conclude that LLM generalization in temporal extraction tasks cannot be predicted from any single dimension alone and cannot be reliably inferred from in-domain or single-dimension evaluations, highlighting the need for reasoning strategies that generalize across dimensions.
中文翻译
时间和事件表达提取是基础的时间推理任务，但由于标注模糊性、领域敏感性和模型行为不稳定，该问题依然困难。现有评估主要关注域内性能，难以揭示分布偏移下的可靠性。我们跨多个模型家族、架构和推理策略，从四个维度系统评估泛化能力，考察基础任务性能迁移、跨维度相关性以及规模、架构和提示策略的影响。这为提示式LLM在时间与事件表达提取任务中的泛化提供了系统研究。我们发现，强基础任务性能通常预测更好的泛化能力，但在显著分布偏移下该关系减弱。归纳提示在领域偏移、对抗扰动、组合性及长度增加中表现最为一致，而规模、架构以及演绎和归纳提示策略带来的增益不均衡且维度特定。结论是，LLM在时间提取任务中的泛化无法仅从单一维度预测，也无法可靠地从域内或单一维度评估推断，凸显了需要跨维度泛化的推理策略的重要性。

CUEing User Simulators: Calibrated User Embeddings for Multi-Turn Benchmarking
CUEing用户模拟器：用于多轮基准测试的校准型用户嵌入
Recent benchmarks rely on user simulators to evaluate AI agents in multi-turn interaction. While existing simulation techniques demonstrate surface fidelity to human style and behavior, ecologically valid interactive benchmarking also requires alignment in when and how agents fail across simulated and real user populations. We find that existing simulators lack outcome calibration: agreement with observed success rates and failure patterns when real users interact with the same agent. We introduce Calibrated User Embeddings (CUE), a framework that both encodes observed sessions and samples continuous representations, then decodes them into persona commands to steer LLMs to act as user simulators without training. Through this, we evaluate user-conditioned replay of past sessions and aggregate metric agreement when sampling novel personas for the same tasks. On $τ^2$-Bench, CUEd simulators commit fewer simulator-attributed errors and more faithfully reproduce real-user agent failure modes, aggregate success rates, and outcomes for specific task-user pairs than other persona-based simulation methods. These gains coexist with competitive user fidelity as measured using metrics established in prior work. After being fit to mostly customer support interactions, the same CUEd simulators generalize to document creation, math tutoring, and casual conversation, and remain effective across different simulator LLMs without CUE retraining.
中文翻译
近期的基准测试依赖用户模拟器来评估AI代理在多轮交互中的表现。尽管现有模拟技术在人类风格和行为上表现出表面相似性，但生态有效性的交互基准测试还需确保代理在模拟与真实用户群体中的失败时机和方式一致。我们发现现有模拟器缺乏结果校准：即当真实用户与同一代理交互时，其成功与失败模式与观察结果不一致。为此，我们引入校准型用户嵌入（CUE），该框架既能编码观测会话，又能采样连续表示，再解码为角色指令，从而无需训练即可引导LLM充当用户模拟器。通过此方法，我们评估了过去会话的用户条件重播以及针对相同任务采样新角色时的聚合指标一致性。在$τ^2$-Bench上，CUEd模拟器产生的模拟器相关错误更少，更真实地再现了真实用户的代理失败模式、聚合成功率以及特定任务-用户组合的结果，优于其他基于角色的模拟方法。这些改进与先前工作中衡量的用户保真度水平相当。在主要基于客服交互训练后，相同的CUEd模拟器可泛化至文档创作、数学辅导和闲聊场景，并在不同模拟器LLM上保持有效性，无需重新训练CUE。
👤 Anjali Kantharuban, Jonas Mueller 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

Counterexample Generation via Per-Theorem Symbolic Verifiers: When Imitation Hurts and Reinforcement Repairs
通过逐定理符号验证器生成反例：模仿有害而强化修复
Large language models often solve a theorem forward yet fail to disprove a closely related false one: a falsification gap that supervised fine-tuning does not close and can actively worsen. We frame counterexample generation as constrained witness emission against a deterministic per-theorem Python verifier, and release SymCE, a corpus of 4,707 false undergraduate-algebra and real-analysis conjectures, each paired with executable verifiers. The verifier also serves as the reward function, making SymCE a training environment. Training Qwen3-4B with SFT followed by GRPO under this oracle reveals an imitation trap: counterexample-only SFT collapses true-theorem recognition from 0.27 to 0.00, while RLVR with a sparse outcome-only reward repairs this and exceeds the base, to 0.66. The collapse replicates across four seeds and on Gemma-3-4B. Sparse and dense rewards yield statistically indistinguishable in-domain success yet diverge by 33 points on a held-out calibration probe, a dissociation we trace to the partial-credit term. Our 4B model outperforms every evaluated 7B open-weights math specialist, remains competitive with six frontier commercial APIs, and transfers under unchanged prompting to GSM8K, MATH-500 and MMLU-college-math. A human audit of 177 verifier decisions finds 97.7% accuracy. Code, data, verifier modules and annotations: https://github.com/ce-rlvr/SymCE.
中文翻译
大型语言模型通常能正向证明一个定理，却无法反驳一个密切相关但错误的命题：这种 falsification gap 无法通过监督微调弥补，甚至可能加剧。我们将反例生成建模为针对确定性逐定理 Python 验证器的约束性证据输出，并发布 SymCE，一个包含 4,707 个错误的本科代数与实分析猜想的数据集，每个猜想均配有可执行验证器。验证器同时作为奖励函数，使 SymCE 成为训练环境。在该 oracle 下对 Qwen3-4B 进行 SFT 后接 GRPO 训练揭示了模仿陷阱：仅生成反例的 SFT 将真命题识别率从 0.27 降至 0.00，而使用稀疏结果奖励的 RLVR 则修复了这一问题，提升至 0.66。该崩溃现象在四个随机种子和 Gemma-3-4B 上均复现。稀疏与密集奖励在域内成功率上无统计差异，但在保留校准探针上相差 33 分，我们将其归因于部分得分项。我们的 4B 模型优于所有评估过的 7B 开源数学专家模型，且与六个前沿商业 API 竞争力相当，并在不改变提示的情况下迁移至 GSM8K、MATH-500 和 MMLU-college-math。对 177 个验证器决策的人工审计显示准确率达 97.7%。代码、数据、验证器模块及标注：https://github.com/ce-rlvr/SymCE。
👤 Omar Farouk Zouak, Houssam Eddine Boukhalfa, Soumaya Lakehal, Shiv Katiyar, Samia Nefti-Meziani 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

Finding the Move Is Not Winning the Game: XiangqiBench for Closed-Loop Evaluation of LLM Agents
找到一步并不等于赢得比赛：面向 LLM 代理的闭环评估的象棋基准 XiangqiBench
Static evaluations credit a language model for naming the right move, but an agent must carry a plan through to a verified outcome while an opponent responds. We introduce XiangqiBench, an executable benchmark that measures this difference in Chinese chess: starting from 119 tactical endgames with forced mates supported by engine or checks-only search, an LLM agent must deliver checkmate against an engine defender. An interactive REPL interface separates real moves, state queries, and forward simulation, and we record 8,568 multi-turn trajectories from 12 frontier LLMs under two observation protocols. Three signals that look like competence each overstate closed-loop success. (i) The Conversion Gap: models play the stored reference first move in 26.1\% of Sighted trials, yet only 13.9\% of these trials end in a win. (ii) The Consistency Gap: the leading model reaches 38.7\% pass@3 but only 5.9\% pass^3, winning all three trials on 7 of the 46 positions it ever wins. (iii) The Simulation Gap: 32.3\% of accepted simulation calls stop on an illegal move, and in 49.3\% of comparable cases the real defender replies differently from the line the agent simulated; self-authored rollouts check legality but cannot anticipate the opponent. Finding the move is not winning the game: agent evaluations should score closed-loop outcomes and report reliability alongside coverage.
中文翻译
静态评估仅认可语言模型命名正确走法，但代理必须在对手响应下持续推进计划并达成经验证的结果。我们引入 XiangqiBench，一个可执行基准，用于衡量中国象棋中的这一差异：从 119 个战术残局（支持引擎或仅检查搜索）出发，LLM 代理需在引擎防守方对抗下实现将死。交互式 REPL 接口分离真实走法、状态查询与前向模拟，我们记录了 12 个前沿 LLM 在两种观察协议下的 8,568 条多轮轨迹。三个看似能力的表现信号均高估了闭环成功。 (i) 转换差距：模型在 26.1% 的可见试验中先走参考第一步，但仅 13.9% 的此类试验最终获胜。 (ii) 一致性差距：领先模型达到 pass@3 为 38.7%，但 pass^3 仅为 5.9%，在它赢下的 46 个位置中，仅有 7 个能连续三步获胜。 (iii) 模拟差距：32.3% 的接受模拟调用在非法走法处终止，且在 49.3% 的可比情况下，真实防守方的回应与代理模拟的路线不同；自编回溯检查合法性，但无法预判对手。找到一步并不等于赢得比赛：代理评估应评分闭环结果，并报告可靠性与覆盖率。

HakemBench: A Turkish Benchmark of Typed Decisions
HakemBench：一种土耳其类型的决策基准
HakemBench is a Turkish benchmark of typed decisions, in which the model under test reads a text, a question and a fixed set of options and returns a probability for every option. Version 1.0 is released fully open under CC BY 4.0, with 2,346 items and 4,275 choice, yes/no and score questions in seven tracks (fact-check triage, education, guardrails, legal routing, moderation, spam and phishing, and customer support). One harness scores decision quality (macro F1), calibration (from the normalised Brier score) and selective automation (from the normalised area under the generalised risk-coverage curve), combines them by a geometric mean and reports intervals from 2,000 bootstrap draws; probes for option order, paraphrase, English translation and substituted names are reported alongside. Most gold labels come from blind passes of one AI model family compared with the votes of a panel of large language models from other model families; they are not human-verified. On a board of 16 rows the leader scores a composite of 0.888 and the lab's own model is 7th at 0.660. Its numbers are not blind. Earlier runs' test results shaped its training data, so its guardrail, moderation and customer support numbers are flagged; with every model scored on the other four tracks only, its composite is 0.678, 6th of 16.
中文翻译
HakemBench 是一个土耳其语决策基准，测试模型需阅读一段文本、一个问题及一组固定选项，并为每个选项返回概率。版本 1.0 完全开源，采用 CC BY 4.0 许可，包含 2,346 个条目，涵盖七个赛道（事实核查分类、教育、护栏、法律路由、内容审核、垃圾与钓鱼信息检测、客户服务），共 4,275 个选择题、是非题和评分题。一个统一评估框架衡量决策质量（宏 F1）、校准度（基于归一化 Brier 分数）和选择性自动化（基于归一化广义风险-覆盖率曲线下面积），通过几何平均合并，并报告 2,000 次自助抽样区间；同时报告选项顺序、改写、英文翻译和替换名称等探测结果。多数黄金标签来自一个 AI 模型家族的盲测结果，与另一组大语言模型家族的投票对比得出，非人工验证。在一个 16 行的榜单中，领先模型综合得分为 0.888，其自身模型位列第 7，得分为 0.660，但其数值并非盲评。早期运行的测试结果影响了其训练数据，因此其护栏、审核与客户支持指标被标记；若仅对其他四个赛道评分，其综合得分为 0.678，16 个模型中排名第 6。

World Embedding Benchmark
世界嵌入基准测试
Physical fidelity has received increasing attention in world models and video generation, yet how video representations encode physical information remains less understood. We introduce the World Embedding Benchmark, comprising 8,000 controlled simulation cases from 80 families spanning fluid mechanics, solid mechanics, dynamics, and optics & electromagnetism. Each case pairs a rendered video with simulation-derived physical annotations, supporting three complementary tasks: text-video retrieval, physical-property regression, and multiple-choice video-description pair classification. We use these tasks to distinguish cross-modal physical alignment from the recoverability of quantitative physical information. Evaluated pre-trained omnimodal embedding models show weak retrieval and near-chance within-family pair classification, while lightweight probes recover useful physical information from frozen video embeddings. Continual contrastive training with physics-specific video-text pairs improves retrieval and pair classification but degrades physical-property regression, revealing a trade-off between alignment and quantitative information recoverability. Finally, we use the embeddings to retrieve reference videos for retrieval-augmented generation with MiniMax-H3. Retrieved references improve the physical fidelity of generated videos, with stronger retrieval models yielding larger gains in our experiments. Together, these findings highlight the need to evaluate physical alignment and property recoverability jointly, and demonstrate the utility of physical representations for improving video generation.
中文翻译
物理保真度在世界模型和视频生成中受到越来越多关注，但视频表示如何编码物理信息仍不明确。我们提出了世界嵌入基准测试，包含来自流体力学、固体力学、动力学以及光学与电磁学等80个领域的8000个受控模拟案例。每个案例都配有一段渲染视频和由模拟生成的物理标注，支持三个互补任务：文本-视频检索、物理属性回归以及多选视频描述对分类。我们利用这些任务区分跨模态物理对齐与定量物理信息的可恢复性。对预训练的多模态嵌入模型的评估显示，其检索能力较弱，且在同族内的配对分类接近随机水平；而轻量级探测器能从冻结的视频嵌入中恢复有用的物理信息。使用特定于物理的视频-文本对进行持续对比训练可提升检索和配对分类性能，但会降低物理属性回归表现，揭示了对齐与定量信息可恢复性之间的权衡。最后，我们利用嵌入检索参考视频以增强生成过程（MiniMax-H3），实验表明检索到的参考视频提升了生成视频的物理保真度，且更强的检索模型带来了更大的性能增益。综合来看，这些发现强调需要联合评估物理对齐与属性可恢复性，并展示了物理表征在改善视频生成中的实用性。

A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control
极低监控读数并非行为控制的证据
Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.
中文翻译
通过可验证奖励进行后训练可能引发奖励黑客行为，从而促使人们将监控机制纳入训练目标，而非仅用于离线审计。我们指出，低监控读数并不能判断此类干预是否真正控制了行为。在一个代码生成环境中，其主要漏洞在推理轨迹开始阶段即可获取，我们训练策略对抗三种通过相同离线门限的监控器：一个域内激活探测器和两个基于策略提前确定最终答案时间的惩罚项。探测器得分从首次记录的训练步骤起就处于数值下限，所有前缀训练运行在终点处的训练得分中位数均为零。这些读数衡量的是不同量，我们未比较其尺度；但在每类监控器内部，低值并不意味着行为控制。在固定配置下，具有相同零中位数训练得分的前缀训练运行，仅因种子不同，其表现范围从混合模式（低黑客比例）到近乎纯奖励黑客模式不等。所有探测器运行均进入黑客模式，但其底层数值读数反映的是探测器验证位置与训练期间读取位置之间的不匹配，而非第二次模糊性。文本层面分析识别出一种前缀失败模式：通用规划和填充模板将漏洞推迟至截断点之后，但并未从最终输出中消除它。因此，低测量承诺不能区分低黑客比例与漏洞承诺延迟。离线判别和低监控对齐读数不足以证明行为控制；必须进行独立的行为检查。我们关注的是终点读数，而非其演化过程。代码已公开于 https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost。

agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Passing the Test You Trained On: Re-evaluating Prompt-Injection Detectors for LLM Agents
通过你训练过的测试：重新评估LLM代理的提示注入检测器
LLM agents increasingly screen tool outputs with small prompt-injection detectors, and teams choose among detectors by their scores on public benchmarks. We ask whether those scores predict how a detector behaves inside an agent. We replay the ground-truth tool calls of two agent benchmarks, AgentDojo and tau-bench, without an LLM to obtain tool outputs that are benign by construction, label injected outputs by differential replay, and evaluate fifteen detectors, including Meta's Prompt Guard 2, and two task-aware LLM judges on these outputs and on the BIPIA benchmark. Detection rankings transfer poorly between benchmarks: the best detector on BIPIA catches 2% of AgentDojo injections at a 1% false-positive rate, and a detector that catches 72% of AgentDojo injections catches 15% on tau-bench. False-positive rates on tool outputs, which range from none to over 90%, do transfer between the two agent benchmarks. Where training data is public, the form of the training inputs explains the results. The BIPIA leader was trained on full BIPIA inputs, but having seen InjecAgent's attack strings as short prompts does not help it find them inside tool outputs; the best detector on both agent benchmarks shares no data with any benchmark and was trained on agent-style inputs. Evaluations meant to inform deployment should use the agent's own tool outputs, report detection at a low false-positive rate, and audit what the detector was trained on.
中文翻译
大型语言模型代理越来越依赖小型提示注入检测器来筛查工具输出，团队通常根据公共基准上的得分选择检测器。我们探讨这些得分是否能预测检测器在代理内部的实际表现。我们重放了两个代理基准AgentDojo和tau-bench的真实工具调用，不使用LLM生成工具输出，使其天然为良性；通过差分重放标记注入输出，并评估了十五个检测器（包括Meta的Prompt Guard 2）以及两个任务感知的LLM裁判在这些输出和BIPIA基准上的表现。检测器的排名在不同基准间转移效果很差：在BIPIA上表现最佳的检测器在AgentDojo上仅以1%误报率捕获2%的注入，在AgentDojo上捕获72%注入的检测器在tau-bench上仅捕获15%。工具输出上的误报率（范围从0到超过90%）在两个代理基准间具有较好的转移性。当训练数据公开时，训练输入的形式可以解释结果。BIPIA领先者是在完整BIPIA输入上训练的，但即使见过InjecAgent的攻击字符串作为短提示，也无法在工具输出中发现它们；在两个代理基准上表现最佳的检测器与任何基准均无数据共享，而是基于代理风格输入训练而成。旨在指导部署的评估应使用代理自身的工具输出，报告低误报率下的检测性能，并审查检测器的训练数据。

Benchmarking Candidate Coverage in Typed Decision Models
类型化决策模型中候选覆盖的基准测试
Typed decision models return choices or distributions over answer options supplied at request time. Accuracy with complete options does not establish whether a model recognizes that a reference answer is missing or avoids rejecting valid candidates. We present a paired candidate-coverage benchmark protocol and an initial evaluation of Laya and Jev across AG News, DBpedia, Emotion, and TREC. The models receive identical frozen texts and requests: 300 calibration and 589 test texts yield 23,932 predictions per model. Present/absent pairs match ordinary candidate count, and name variants preserve descriptions, members, and order. Native rejection behavior differs sharply: at five TREC candidates with natural names, Laya detects 97.2% of missing-answer cases but falsely rejects 69.7% of present controls; Jev's rates are 24.8% and 0.0%. Calibration-only none-score thresholds change these rates to 33.9%/3.7% and 45.0%/1.8%, respectively. On DBpedia, Jev's high coverage-score AUROC supports a stronger operating point, whereas both models have weak complete-set accuracy on Emotion. Competence-conditioned analysis, probability-precision sensitivity, and interface audits show why classification, score ranking, and rejection policies need separate measurement. This initial benchmark is descriptive and limited to reference-label omission; it does not establish natural out-of-scope generalization, causal mechanisms, or a new rejection method.
中文翻译
类型化决策模型在请求时返回选项或答案分布。仅在完整选项集下准确，并不能说明模型是否识别出参考答案缺失，或是否避免拒绝有效候选。我们提出了一种成对候选覆盖基准协议，并对Laya和Jev在AG News、DBpedia、Emotion和TREC上的表现进行了初步评估。模型接收相同的冻结文本和请求：300个校准文本和589个测试文本，每模型产生23,932个预测。存在/缺失配对匹配普通候选数量，名称变体保留描述、成员和顺序。原生拒绝行为差异显著：在五个自然命名的TREC候选情况下，Laya检测到97.2%的缺失答案情况，但错误拒绝了69.7%的现存对照；Jev的对应率为24.8%和0.0%。仅使用校准的非得分阈值将这些比率调整为33.9%/3.7%和45.0%/1.8%。在DBpedia上，Jev的高覆盖率AUROC支持更优的操作点，而两模型在Emotion上的完整集准确性均较弱。基于能力的分析、概率-精度敏感性以及接口审计表明，分类、得分排序和拒绝策略需分别测量。该初步基准为描述性，且仅限于参考标签遗漏；它无法建立自然的域外泛化、因果机制或新的拒绝方法。

Benchmarking Literature Retrieval for a Model Organism: A Dictyostelium Case Study
面向模式生物的文献检索基准测试：一个黏菌案例研究
Biological literature retrieval systems are often developed and evaluated using broad biomedical corpora and general-purpose search tasks. However, many curated knowledge bases operate in narrower model-organism domains, where the literature is sparse and terminology is organism-specific. We introduce a retrieval benchmark from dictyBase for Dictyostelium, a model organism in cell and developmental biology. The benchmark consists of curator-generated biological queries linked to PubMed-indexed articles, together with structured gene annotations. Using this benchmark, we study three factors in niche biological retrieval: cross-encoder reranking, gene-aware query expansion, and abstract-only versus full-text retrieval. We report that reranking and gene-aware query expansion improve retrieval selectively: reranking is most useful when the model is well suited to biological evidence matching, whereas curated annotations help clarify compact biological queries by reducing vocabulary mismatch. Full-text chunks substantially improve retrieval when abstracts omit supporting evidence, increasing both candidate recall and top-rank performance, although these cases are harder than queries supported by abstracts. Data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/fulaibaowang/dictycite, and the benchmark dataset is additionally archived on Zenodo.
中文翻译
生物文献检索系统通常使用广泛的生物医学语料库和通用搜索任务进行开发与评估。然而，许多经过精心整理的知识库运行在更狭窄的模式生物领域，其文献稀疏且术语具有生物体特异性。我们为黏菌（Dictyostelium）引入了一个来自dictyBase的检索基准。该基准包含由研究人员生成的生物学查询及其对应的PubMed索引文章，以及结构化的基因注释。利用这一基准，我们研究了三个在特定生物检索中的因素：交叉编码重排序、基因感知的查询扩展，以及仅摘要与全文检索的对比。结果表明，重排序和基因感知的查询扩展在特定情况下能提升检索效果：当模型擅长匹配生物证据时，重排序最为有效；而经过人工标注的注释可通过减少词汇不匹配来澄清简洁的生物查询。当摘要中缺少支持性证据时，使用全文片段可显著提升检索性能，既提高了候选召回率也提升了排名表现，尽管这类查询比有摘要支持的查询更难处理。数据和代码已公开于 https://github.com/fulaibaowang/dictycite，基准数据集亦存档于Zenodo。
👤 Yun Wang, Gad Shaulsky, Tomaž Curk, Blaž Zupan 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03095]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models
异构AI模型间的同伴影响
When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.
中文翻译
当两个AI代理意见相左时，谁说服了谁？随着多智能体系统越来越多地整合不同家族和规模的语言模型，这一问题决定了哪些判断能在交互中留存。我们将说服力定义为代理在与持异议同伴进行一次交流后决策概率分布的变化，测试了七种开源权重模型在三个语言理解任务上的表现。我们发现说服力很强：当模型意见不一致时，接收方往往在看到同伴的答案和解释后放弃原有判断。令人惊讶的是，独立确定性或模型规模都无法可靠预测说服动态。在孤立状态下几乎完全一致的模型可能最容易被说服，而小型模型也能与大型模型一样有效地作为说服者，并同样有效地抵抗影响。此外，我们发现变化幅度更多取决于听者的敏感性而非说者的说服力。因此，说服模式因模型组合而异，异质性在某些组合中会放大说服力，在另一些组合中则抑制说服力，使得运行小型模型的异议代理能够推翻大型模型的判断。这些发现表明，交互模型的行为无法从其个体属性推断，必须在其实际运行组合中进行评估。
👤 Frida Nøhr Laustsen, Marie Haahr Petersen, Victoria Popa, Ariel Flint, Romualdo Pastor-Satorras et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03080]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
MintEval: Do LLMs Implement the Trading Strategy You Asked For? A Behavioural-Equivalence Benchmark for Natural-Language-to-Strategy Code
MintEval：大语言模型是否实现了你要求的交易策略？一种自然语言到策略代码的行为等价基准
Large language models are moving from producing trading signals to writing the code that executes them. The failure mode of the second role is silent: generated code runs, a backtest plots, yet the risk logic that the trader described is not the logic being executed. Existing code benchmarks test functional correctness on unit tests and finance benchmarks test forecasting; neither measures whether an implementation behaves like the strategy that was asked for. We introduce MintEval, a benchmark in which reference strategies are generated programmatically from a library of composable building blocks, back-translated into colloquial trader instructions, and re-implemented by the model under test. Generated and reference programs are executed bar by bar on identical market data and frictions, and compared on their actions rather than on code similarity or profit: alpha is differenced away. MintEval v0 contains 800 tasks on BTCUSDT 15-minute data, stratified by an execution-measured state-span complexity tau that is decoupled from description length. Low-cost models reach a mean ActionMatch of at most 0.544 and reproduce at most 0.087 of tasks exactly; on a stratified subset of 200 tasks a frontier model (Claude Opus 5.5) reaches 0.889 and reproduces 0.575 exactly, yet still fails silently on 0.275 of tasks. Given a menu of building blocks, models identify the strategy almost perfectly, yet 79.2% of the implementations whose specification was read correctly diverge on more than 10% of active bars. The LLM judge of a recent strategy-generation benchmark, applied verbatim, accepts every one of these silent failures.
中文翻译
大语言模型正从生成交易信号转向编写执行这些策略的代码。第二种角色的失败模式是无声的：生成的代码可以运行，回测图表也能绘制，但执行的风险逻辑与交易员描述的并不一致。现有的代码基准测试关注单元测试的功能正确性，金融基准则关注预测能力；两者均未衡量实现是否与所要求的策略行为一致。我们提出MintEval，一个基准测试，其中参考策略通过一组可组合的构建模块程序化生成，再反向翻译为通俗的交易员指令，由待测模型重新实现。生成的程序与参考程序在相同的市场数据和摩擦条件下逐根K线执行，并通过行为对比而非代码相似性或收益差异进行比较：Alpha被差分消除。MintEval v0 包含800个任务，基于BTCUSDT 15分钟数据，按执行测量的状态跨度复杂度tau分层，该指标与描述长度解耦。低成本模型平均动作匹配度仅为0.544，仅能精确复现0.087的任务；在200个分层子任务上，前沿模型（Claude Opus 5.5）达到0.889的动作匹配度，精确复现0.575，但仍对0.275的任务出现无声失败。给定一组构建模块，模型几乎完美识别策略，但79.2%的实现虽正确读取了规范，却在超过10%的活跃K线上发生偏差。最近一项策略生成基准中的LLM评判器原样应用时，接受了所有这些无声失败。

Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time
Sentry：在测试时学习从大语言模型智能体失败中恢复
LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context. Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体常因无效工具调用、重复动作或缺乏依据的推理而在任务中途失败，从这些失败中学习是提升可靠性的路径。我们发现，失败知识如何传递给智能体与知识本身同样重要。失败教训具有条件性：若保留在智能体的上下文中，当失败未发生时会误触发；而从不断演化的策略手册中移除它们反而能提升性能。相比之下，运行时干预仅在失败发生时起作用，但无法从修复中学习。我们认为失败知识是条件性知识，应有条件地暴露，并在Sentry中实现了这一原则——一个与智能体并行运行的失败管理层。当Sentry检测到失败时，它会从外部策略手册中检索匹配的教训以指导恢复，无需访问任务奖励即可验证智能体是否成功恢复，并仅在恢复成功时存储新教训；整个策略手册始终不进入智能体的上下文。在多个智能体基准测试中，Sentry在所有基准上均优于最强的运行时干预基线（平均提升37%），在两个可对比的基准上优于最强的上下文演化基线（提升39%）；将Sentry与上下文演化结合可进一步提升性能。学习到的教训可迁移至未见任务，受控实验表明，即使相关教训仍可按需获取，将完整策略手册暴露给智能体也会降低性能。
👤 Changxiu Ji, Amy Lu, Qizheng Zhang, Kunle Olukotun 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Continual Graph Memory for Mathematical Research Agents
持续图记忆：面向数学研究智能体的构建
Using frontier agent harnesses to tackle mathematical research problems has emerged as an effective means of advancing mathematics. However, solving frontier problems in mathematics may require a massive number of agents working in parallel for extended periods to construct proofs, thereby generating an enormous volume of intermediate proof results. Organizing these intermediate results throughout a long-horizon proof-search process and reusing knowledge gained from prior explorations remain major challenges. We present Ansatz, a mathematical research agent built around Continual Graph Memory, a graph-based, evolvable, cross-problem mathematical research memory system that explicitly organizes the entire proof search process and reuses information from exploration trajectories of previous problems. Specifically, we develop a unified graph memory that represents all intermediate exploration results, including facts, plans, and counterexamples, together with edges that explicitly represent the relationships among them; dependency-aware retrieval supplies precisely targeted local context; an evidence-sensitive curator updates the research frontier and distills lessons from prior attempts; and scoped recall surfaces earlier statements and negative findings for local re-proving rather than uncritical reuse. Experiments cover runs across all ten First Proof Second Batch problems, together with four component studies. Ansatz reports closure on all ten research tasks, demonstrating its ability to sustain and resume long-horizon mathematical search. Beyond these problems, Ansatz also produces solutions to the Jamison caterpillar conjecture and Erdős Problems 289, 348, and 488 without human intervention, and makes partial progress on several open problems, illustrating its strong ability to solve open mathematical research problems.
中文翻译
使用前沿智能体钩子解决数学研究问题已成为推动数学进展的有效手段。然而，解决数学前沿问题可能需要大量智能体并行工作较长时间以构建证明，从而产生海量中间证明结果。在整个长时程证明搜索过程中组织这些中间结果，并重用先前探索所获得的知识，仍是重大挑战。我们提出Ansatz，一种基于持续图记忆的数学研究智能体，该记忆系统为基于图结构、可演化、跨问题的数学研究记忆体系，显式组织整个证明搜索过程，并重用先前问题探索轨迹中的信息。具体而言，我们开发了一个统一的图记忆，表示所有中间探索结果，包括事实、计划和反例，并通过边显式表达其相互关系；依赖感知的检索提供精确的目标局部上下文；证据敏感的策展人更新研究前沿并提炼先前尝试的经验；作用域召回则呈现早期陈述和负面发现，用于局部重新证明而非盲目复用。实验覆盖全部十个First Proof Second Batch问题，以及四项组件研究。Ansatz成功完成所有十项研究任务，展示了其维持和恢复长时程数学搜索的能力。此外，Ansatz在无人干预下解决了Jamison毛虫猜想及Erdős问题289、348和488，并在多个开放问题上取得部分进展，充分展现了其解决开放数学研究问题的强大能力。

Probe the Harness: Setup Checks for Stale-Data RL Comparisons in Language Models
探查钩子：语言模型中过时数据强化学习比较的设置检查
Methods for training language models on stale samples are judged by comparisons against importance-corrected baselines. We show that details of the experimental harness can reverse the observed ranking of methods, and we introduce PTH (Probe The Harness), a set of checks that makes the harness visible. Our case is a comparison between SAN, a behaviour-free method, and truncated importance sampling (TIS) on verl and in a single-GPU trainer, in which SAN first finished ahead in both stacks. Four details of the harness changed this comparison: the PPO ratio was taken against the learner's own recomputed probabilities, the data seed did not reach the TIS arm, the replay queue reused its first batch for 33 updates, and two loss normalisers differed from their description. In each case the logged quantity looked consistent with a working setup, while the quantity that defines the comparison went unchecked. With the harness checked, TIS matches SAN on verl, and in the trainer TIS learns steadily while SAN keeps a margin. We contribute the signature of each detail and its effect on the comparison, reference results for TIS and uncorrected GRPO under sampler lag, and the PTH checklist.
中文翻译
在过时样本上训练语言模型的方法通过与重要性校正基线的比较来评估。我们展示实验钩子的细节可能导致方法排名反转，并引入PTH（Probe The Harness）——一套使钩子可见的检查清单。案例涉及SAN（一种无行为方法）与截断重要性采样（TIS）在verl和单GPU训练器上的比较，其中SAN在两个堆栈中均率先完成。四个钩子细节改变了这一比较：PPO比率是基于学习者自身重新计算的概率；数据种子未到达TIS分支；重放队列在其前33次更新中重复使用首个批次；两个损失归一化器与其描述不符。在每种情况下，记录量看似符合正常设置，但决定比较的关键量却未被核查。在检查钩子后，TIS在verl上与SAN持平，在训练器中TIS稳定学习，而SAN保持领先优势。我们贡献了每个细节的特征及其对比较的影响，提供了TIS和未校正GRPO在采样延迟下的参考结果，以及PTH检查清单。

ROUTEAUDIT: Interaction-Aware Identification for Budgeted Multi-Verifier Routing
ROUTEAUDIT：面向预算型多验证器路由的交互感知识别
Adaptive multi-verifier systems are commonly compared through endpoint quality-cost gaps, even when the verifier catalog, availability, accounting, information filtration, or scorer changes with the policy. We formulate verifier routing as a contract-conditioned identification problem. The contract records request support, verifier catalog, realized availability, resource accounting, online filtration, and post-trace scoring; a matched route contrast changes only the policy coordinate. ROUTEAUDIT adds three measurable objects to this contract. A contract lattice averages coordinate increments over every admissible bridge order and reports the resulting attribution together with its path sensitivity. A policy-independent response tape identifies paired sequential contrasts when adaptive policies reveal different observations. For incomplete matching, request-level bounds use whichever potential outcome remains observed and give a sharp finite-population interval. The protocol commits paid observations and ledger events before the oracle join and returns an attribution certificate for each comparison. On two held-out raw-tail caches, matched static SF+SA equals the cascade, assigning the apparent gains of 0.1797 and 0.1250 over full static to the verifier-set edge. On 1,319 held-out task requests, the learned and RLVR studies report quality 0.9522 and 0.9553 versus 0.9484 for matched static; the RLVR-static paired difference is +0.0068 with a request-paired interval $[0.0015,0.0122]$ and a training-seed-by-request hierarchical interval $[0.0006,0.0131]$. Controlled attribution recovery yields route mean absolute error 0.0011 and endpoint reconstruction error 0.0004. Factorial, bridge-order, and stochastic-provider studies evaluate the certificate interface; RLVR supplies a learned-policy stress test under the same identification contract.
中文翻译
自适应多验证器系统通常通过端点质量-成本差距进行比较，即使验证器目录、可用性、会计、信息过滤或评分器随策略变化。我们将验证器路由建模为合同条件下的识别问题。合同记录请求支持、验证器目录、实际可用性、资源会计、在线过滤和事后评分；匹配路径对比仅改变策略坐标。ROUTEAUDIT在此合同中添加了三个可度量对象。合同格网对所有可接受的桥接顺序的坐标增量取平均，并报告结果归因及其路径敏感性。策略无关响应带识别出当自适应策略揭示不同观察时的成对序列对比。对于不完全匹配，请求级边界采用仍可观察的潜在结果，并给出精确的有限总体区间。该协议在Oracle连接前承诺支付观察和账本事件，并为每次比较返回归因证书。在两个保留的原始尾部缓存上，匹配的静态SF+SA等于级联，将全静态的明显收益0.1797和0.1250归因于验证器集边缘。在1,319个保留任务请求上，学习和RLVR研究报告的质量分别为0.9522和0.9553，而匹配静态为0.9484；RLVR-静态配对差异为+0.0068，请求配对区间为[0.0015,0.0122]，训练种子-请求分层区间为[0.0006,0.0131]。受控归因恢复得到路径均方误差0.0011和端点重建误差0.0004。因子、桥接顺序和随机提供者研究评估了证书接口；RLVR在相同识别合同下提供了学习策略的压力测试。

VERSE: Verified Self-Evolving Optimizer for Agent Harnesses
VERSE：用于智能体集成的验证自演化优化器
Harness evolution improves an LLM agent's prompts, tools, and workflow, while the optimizer's own tools and procedures often remain fixed. We study whether an optimizer can improve another agent more effectively by also improving how it diagnoses failures, develops edits, and tests their effects. Two observations guide our design. In a controlled study, optimizer self-evolution fails to improve performance without execution-based verification, but achieves the best result of that study when verification is available. Across five executors, self-evolving optimizers build their own tools for failure analysis, verification, training audits, and workflow control. Motivated by these findings, we introduce VERSE, a Verified Self-Evolving optimizer for agent harnesses. VERSE lets the optimizer test draft edits, replay failures, and perturb suspected steps before submission, while tracking fixes and regressions across rounds. Using this feedback, the optimizer revises both the executor harness and its own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes, while the weights of the optimizer and executor models stay fixed. Under a shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks, VERSE improves all four evaluated harness optimizers on held-out SWE-rebench tasks and newer out-of-distribution tasks in five languages. Its best validation-selected harness reaches 42.3% and 37.7% accuracy, respectively, against 39.2% and 29.3% for the strongest baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/wzekai/VERSE.

集成演化可改进LLM智能体的提示、工具与工作流，但优化器自身的工具与流程通常保持不变。我们研究优化器是否可通过同时改进其故障诊断、编辑制定与效果测试方式，更有效地优化其他智能体。两项观察指导我们的设计：在受控研究中，若无基于执行的验证，优化器自演化无法提升性能；但当验证可用时，其达到该研究的最佳结果。在五个执行器上，自演化优化器构建了用于故障分析、验证、训练审计与工作流控制的自有工具。基于这些发现，我们提出VERSE——一种用于智能体集成的验证自演化优化器。VERSE允许优化器测试草稿修改、重放故障、扰动可疑步骤后再提交，同时追踪各轮次中的修复与退化。利用此反馈，优化器不仅修订执行器集成，还同时优化其自身提示、技能、工具、钩子与笔记，而优化器与执行器模型权重保持不变。在共享协议下，使用分离的训练、验证与测试任务，VERSE在保留的SWE-rebench任务及五种语言的新分布外任务上均优于所有评估过的集成优化器。其最佳验证选中集成分别达到42.3%和37.7%的准确率，优于最强基线的39.2%和29.3%。代码已公开于 https://github.com/wzekai/VERSE。
