Predicting and Repairing Merge Collapse in Large Language Models
预测并修复大型语言模型中的合并坍缩
Large language models fine-tuned from a shared base can be merged by averaging their task vectors, but some merges collapse far below the base model, and common merge operators give no warning before evaluation. We show that one statistic of the specialists' task vectors both predicts this collapse and calibrates its repair. The power that averaging removes equals the variance of the task vectors across specialists, our measure of interference. Under a working noise model, the disturbance that a merge injects grows with the merge coefficient and with interference, yielding a pre-merge score. In our experiments on twenty-two merge configurations from four model families, only destructive merges exceed a threshold on this score. We find that statistics of sign conflict between specialists, a common target of existing merge operators, are anti-predictive. We then predicted the outcomes of fourteen merges before evaluating them, and twelve predictions were correct, including the destructive outcome of a specialist pair pushed past the threshold by continued pretraining. To address this collapse, we introduce PRISM, an operator that averages the task vectors first and then soft-thresholds each layer at a level set by the layer's interference. Without data or tuning, PRISM keeps all five destructive merges above the threshold within evaluation noise of the base model, where plain averaging falls at least 14.4 points below it or collapses entirely. We apply PRISM only above the threshold and keep the plain average for merges below it, which include all fifteen harmless ones. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/PRISM.
中文翻译
从共享基础模型微调得到的大型语言模型可通过平均任务向量进行合并，但某些合并会远低于基础模型性能，且常见合并操作在评估前无法预警。我们发现专家任务向量的一个统计量既能预测这种坍缩，又能校准其修复。平均操作所移除的性能等于专家间任务向量的方差，即我们衡量干扰的指标。在工作噪声模型下，合并引入的扰动随合并系数和干扰程度增加，从而得出合并前评分。在来自四个模型家族的二十二种合并配置实验中，仅有破坏性合并超过该评分阈值。我们发现，现有合并操作常关注的专家间符号冲突统计量反而具有反向预测性。随后我们提前预测了十四次合并的结果，其中十二次正确，包括一个因持续预训练而超出阈值的专家对的破坏性结果。为解决此坍缩问题，我们提出 PRISM，一种先平均任务向量再按各层干扰水平进行软阈值处理的合并算子。无需数据或调优，PRISM 使所有五个破坏性合并保持在评估噪声范围内接近基础模型，而普通平均法至少低于基础模型14.4分或完全坍缩。我们仅在超过阈值时使用 PRISM，低于阈值的合并仍采用普通平均法，其中包括全部十五个无害合并。代码已公开于 https://github.com/js-lee-AI/PRISM。
👤 Jungseob Lee, Seungyoon Lee, Sugyeong Eo, Hyeonseok Moon, Jaehyung Seo et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rl
Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective
在线策略蒸馏中的收益与坍缩：强化学习视角
On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at https://github.com/HancCui/opd_hacking.
中文翻译
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）已成为语言模型后训练的重要方法。然而，尽管其带来性能提升，OPD 也可能导致生成内容过度冗长重复的坍缩现象，其背后机制尚不清晰。我们从强化学习视角解释这一现象：教师模型隐式奖励学生行为，即使这些行为它自身极少表现出。基于此视角，我们的实验表明，OPD 提升性能但并未扩展学生的实际能力。当隐式奖励模型可靠时，OPD 使正确响应更容易被采样；反之，当偏好与质量错位时，会出现奖励劫持：隐式奖励模型放大了学生冗长重复的生成，尽管教师本身几乎不生成此类文本。基于此诊断，我们发现训练期间屏蔽不良响应以及使用 SFT 初始化均可有效缓解坍缩。综合来看，这些发现表明 OPD 放大了教师隐式反馈所偏好的学生行为，焦点从教师生成质量转向其对学生轨迹评估的可靠性。
👤 Han Cui, Jianhao Yan, Yun Luo, Hongbo Zhang, Zhizhang Fu et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03095]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models
异构AI模型间的同伴影响
When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.
中文翻译
当两个AI代理意见相左时，谁说服了谁？随着多智能体系统越来越多地整合不同家族和规模的语言模型，这一问题决定了哪些判断能在交互中留存。我们将说服力定义为代理在与持异议同伴进行一次交流后决策概率分布的变化，测试了七种开源权重模型在三个语言理解任务上的表现。我们发现说服力很强：当模型意见不一致时，接收方往往在看到同伴的答案和解释后放弃原有判断。令人惊讶的是，独立确定性或模型规模都无法可靠预测说服动态。在孤立状态下几乎完全一致的模型可能最容易被说服，而小型模型也能与大型模型一样有效地作为说服者，并同样有效地抵抗影响。此外，我们发现变化幅度更多取决于听者的敏感性而非说者的说服力。因此，说服模式因模型组合而异，异质性在某些组合中会放大说服力，在另一些组合中则抑制说服力，使得运行小型模型的异议代理能够推翻大型模型的判断。这些发现表明，交互模型的行为无法从其个体属性推断，必须在其实际运行组合中进行评估。
👤 Frida Nøhr Laustsen, Marie Haahr Petersen, Victoria Popa, Ariel Flint, Romualdo Pastor-Satorras et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

MintEval: Do LLMs Implement the Trading Strategy You Asked For? A Behavioural-Equivalence Benchmark for Natural-Language-to-Strategy Code
MintEval：大语言模型是否实现了你要求的交易策略？一种自然语言到策略代码的行为等价基准
Large language models are moving from producing trading signals to writing the code that executes them. The failure mode of the second role is silent: generated code runs, a backtest plots, yet the risk logic that the trader described is not the logic being executed. Existing code benchmarks test functional correctness on unit tests and finance benchmarks test forecasting; neither measures whether an implementation behaves like the strategy that was asked for. We introduce MintEval, a benchmark in which reference strategies are generated programmatically from a library of composable building blocks, back-translated into colloquial trader instructions, and re-implemented by the model under test. Generated and reference programs are executed bar by bar on identical market data and frictions, and compared on their actions rather than on code similarity or profit: alpha is differenced away. MintEval v0 contains 800 tasks on BTCUSDT 15-minute data, stratified by an execution-measured state-span complexity tau that is decoupled from description length. Low-cost models reach a mean ActionMatch of at most 0.544 and reproduce at most 0.087 of tasks exactly; on a stratified subset of 200 tasks a frontier model (Claude Opus 5.5) reaches 0.889 and reproduces 0.575 exactly, yet still fails silently on 0.275 of tasks. Given a menu of building blocks, models identify the strategy almost perfectly, yet 79.2% of the implementations whose specification was read correctly diverge on more than 10% of active bars. The LLM judge of a recent strategy-generation benchmark, applied verbatim, accepts every one of these silent failures.
中文翻译
大语言模型正从生成交易信号转向编写执行这些策略的代码。第二种角色的失败模式是无声的：生成的代码可以运行，回测图表也能绘制，但执行的风险逻辑与交易员描述的并不一致。现有的代码基准测试关注单元测试的功能正确性，金融基准则关注预测能力；两者均未衡量实现是否与所要求的策略行为一致。我们提出MintEval，一个基准测试，其中参考策略通过一组可组合的构建模块程序化生成，再反向翻译为通俗的交易员指令，由待测模型重新实现。生成的程序与参考程序在相同的市场数据和摩擦条件下逐根K线执行，并通过行为对比而非代码相似性或收益差异进行比较：Alpha被差分消除。MintEval v0 包含800个任务，基于BTCUSDT 15分钟数据，按执行测量的状态跨度复杂度tau分层，该指标与描述长度解耦。低成本模型平均动作匹配度仅为0.544，仅能精确复现0.087的任务；在200个分层子任务上，前沿模型（Claude Opus 5.5）达到0.889的动作匹配度，精确复现0.575，但仍对0.275的任务出现无声失败。给定一组构建模块，模型几乎完美识别策略，但79.2%的实现虽正确读取了规范，却在超过10%的活跃K线上发生偏差。最近一项策略生成基准中的LLM评判器原样应用时，接受了所有这些无声失败。

SecJev: Bringing Security Expertise to System One Decision Models
SecJev：将安全专业知识引入系统一决策模型
Security workflows need models that turn complex observations and explicit policies into decisions. System One models introduced by Jev return typed predictions and probabilities; security specialization supplies the domain expertise behind those predictions. We introduce SecJev, to our knowledge the first family of Jev-like decision models specialized for security, spanning 0.8B to 9B parameters. Built on Kev's single-pass candidate scorer, SecJev learns Boolean, choice, and ordered decisions from text, telemetry, and observation histories. We develop SecJev-Corpus to unify source-label prediction and explicit-policy evaluation across 14 tasks and eight sources. It covers tool outputs, traffic, federated updates, consensus, authentication, and vehicle messages. Scene-weighted training adapts the models across these domains while preserving a shared typed decision interface. Security specialization improves every model in the family; SecJev-0.8B outperforms general Kev-9B by 20.51 percentage points in task-macro accuracy. Comparisons with answer-only generative fine-tuning show close accuracy and latency with lower peak inference memory. Tests on new source groups reproduce gains over Kev in prompt-injection and traffic decisions, with capture-dependent false alarms. We release adapters, decision heads, SecJev-Corpus, and training and inference code.
中文翻译
安全工作流需要能够将复杂的观测结果和明确的策略转化为决策的模型。Jev提出的系统一模型返回类型化预测和概率；安全专业化则提供了这些预测背后领域的专业知识。我们引入SecJev，据我们所知，首个专用于安全领域的Jev类决策模型家族，涵盖0.8B至9B参数规模。基于Kev的单次候选评分器，SecJev从文本、遥测数据和观察历史中学习布尔型、选择型和有序型决策。我们构建了SecJev-Corpus，统一了14项任务和8个数据源中的源标签预测与显式策略评估，涵盖工具输出、流量、联邦更新、共识、认证及车辆消息。场景加权训练使模型在各领域间自适应，同时保持共享的类型化决策接口。安全专业化提升了该家族中每个模型的表现；SecJev-0.8B在任务宏准确率上比通用Kev-9B高出20.51个百分点。与仅回答生成微调的对比显示，其准确率和延迟接近，但峰值推理内存更低。在新源组上的测试重现了对Kev在提示注入和流量决策上的优势，但存在依赖捕获的误报。我们发布了适配器、决策头、SecJev-Corpus以及训练和推理代码。

Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time
Sentry：在测试时学习从大语言模型智能体失败中恢复
LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context. Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体常因无效工具调用、重复动作或缺乏依据的推理而在任务中途失败，从这些失败中学习是提升可靠性的路径。我们发现，失败知识如何传递给智能体与知识本身同样重要。失败教训具有条件性：若保留在智能体的上下文中，当失败未发生时会误触发；而从不断演化的策略手册中移除它们反而能提升性能。相比之下，运行时干预仅在失败发生时起作用，但无法从修复中学习。我们认为失败知识是条件性知识，应有条件地暴露，并在Sentry中实现了这一原则——一个与智能体并行运行的失败管理层。当Sentry检测到失败时，它会从外部策略手册中检索匹配的教训以指导恢复，无需访问任务奖励即可验证智能体是否成功恢复，并仅在恢复成功时存储新教训；整个策略手册始终不进入智能体的上下文。在多个智能体基准测试中，Sentry在所有基准上均优于最强的运行时干预基线（平均提升37%），在两个可对比的基准上优于最强的上下文演化基线（提升39%）；将Sentry与上下文演化结合可进一步提升性能。学习到的教训可迁移至未见任务，受控实验表明，即使相关教训仍可按需获取，将完整策略手册暴露给智能体也会降低性能。
👤 Changxiu Ji, Amy Lu, Qizheng Zhang, Kunle Olukotun 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Probe the Harness: Setup Checks for Stale-Data RL Comparisons in Language Models
探查钩子：语言模型中过时数据强化学习比较的设置检查
Methods for training language models on stale samples are judged by comparisons against importance-corrected baselines. We show that details of the experimental harness can reverse the observed ranking of methods, and we introduce PTH (Probe The Harness), a set of checks that makes the harness visible. Our case is a comparison between SAN, a behaviour-free method, and truncated importance sampling (TIS) on verl and in a single-GPU trainer, in which SAN first finished ahead in both stacks. Four details of the harness changed this comparison: the PPO ratio was taken against the learner's own recomputed probabilities, the data seed did not reach the TIS arm, the replay queue reused its first batch for 33 updates, and two loss normalisers differed from their description. In each case the logged quantity looked consistent with a working setup, while the quantity that defines the comparison went unchecked. With the harness checked, TIS matches SAN on verl, and in the trainer TIS learns steadily while SAN keeps a margin. We contribute the signature of each detail and its effect on the comparison, reference results for TIS and uncorrected GRPO under sampler lag, and the PTH checklist.
中文翻译
在过时样本上训练语言模型的方法通过与重要性校正基线的比较来评估。我们展示实验钩子的细节可能导致方法排名反转，并引入PTH（Probe The Harness）——一套使钩子可见的检查清单。案例涉及SAN（一种无行为方法）与截断重要性采样（TIS）在verl和单GPU训练器上的比较，其中SAN在两个堆栈中均率先完成。四个钩子细节改变了这一比较：PPO比率是基于学习者自身重新计算的概率；数据种子未到达TIS分支；重放队列在其前33次更新中重复使用首个批次；两个损失归一化器与其描述不符。在每种情况下，记录量看似符合正常设置，但决定比较的关键量却未被核查。在检查钩子后，TIS在verl上与SAN持平，在训练器中TIS稳定学习，而SAN保持领先优势。我们贡献了每个细节的特征及其对比较的影响，提供了TIS和未校正GRPO在采样延迟下的参考结果，以及PTH检查清单。
👤 Taiheng Pan 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

To Explore The Strange New World Beyond Data Distribution: System Behavior, Causality Tax, and Non-causal Base Model
探索数据分布之外的奇异新世界：系统行为、因果税与非因果基础模型
We show that the causality of language models (LMs) may not be necessary nor optimal. This is the case when system behavior (denoted as $S$) is incorporated as a first-principle Bayesian feature. Here, $S$ refers to extra dominant factors beyond the data space, and they involve coupled effects. Despite being the de facto foundation of modern architecture, recent studies indicate persistent mismatches and contradictions with causality. These issues largely stem from system behavior rather than the data distribution. We therefore propose the SBD framework, which incorporates $S$ as an irreducible component of the evidence lower bound (ELBO). SBD theoretically reveals a counter-intuitive Causality Tax phenomenon, where causality emerges as a suboptimal approximation with an additional structural error, due to the obliviousness to $S$. To address the challenge of latent variable analysis, we validate the SBD-predicted impact of $S$ via implicit measurements, theoretical-bound-guided controls, and Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) evaluations. In particular, we construct Green Shell (GSH) to show the possibility of reducing Causality Tax. GSH is a non-causal variational family, and it replaces the sequential dependency chain of $S$ components with a divide-and-conquer partition. NTK spectra in the lazy-training regime confirm that GSH always achieves significantly tighter error bounds than causality, with $7dB+$ improvement in signal-to-noise ratio. In the relatively later stage of lazy-training, GSH further leads to superior generalization (up to $20\%$ richer multi-scale fitting capabilities). Taken together, SBD establishes system behavior as a complementary theoretical abstraction besides causality and distribution fitting, opening new research avenues such as designing and optimizing LM base models.
中文翻译
我们表明，语言模型（LMs）的因果性可能并非必要，也非最优。当系统行为（记为$S$）作为第一性原理贝叶斯特征纳入时，情况即如此。此处$S$指超越数据空间的额外主导因素，且涉及耦合效应。尽管作为现代架构的默认基础，近期研究表明其与因果性存在持续的不匹配与矛盾。这些问题主要源于系统行为而非数据分布。因此我们提出SBD框架，将$S$作为证据下界（ELBO）中不可约的组成部分。SBD理论揭示了一种反直觉的“因果税”现象：由于对$S$的忽视，因果性成为次优近似，伴随额外的结构性误差。为应对潜在变量分析挑战，我们通过隐式测量、理论边界引导控制和神经正切核（NTK）评估验证了SBD对$S$影响的预测。特别地，我们构建了Green Shell（GSH），展示了降低因果税的可能性。GSH是一种非因果变分族，它用分治分区替代$S$组件的序列依赖链。在懒惰训练阶段的NTK谱分析确认，GSH始终比因果性实现显著更紧的误差界，信噪比提升达7dB以上；在相对后期的懒惰训练阶段，GSH进一步带来更优泛化能力（多尺度拟合能力最高提升20%）。综上，SBD将系统行为确立为与因果性和分布拟合并列的互补理论抽象，开辟了设计和优化LM基础模型的新研究路径。

FSPO: Policy-Consistent Risk and Pareto-Feasible Control for Budgeted LLM RL Post-Training
FSPO：面向预算型大语言模型强化学习后训练的策略一致风险与帕累托可行控制
Adaptive LLM reinforcement-learning post-training changes multiple training actuators online, including rollout temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, and update budget. Three coupled issues remain unresolved. A future-risk model trained from behavior trajectories need not estimate the risk induced by the controller that will be deployed; a score calibrated on logged state-action pairs can become miscalibrated after selective action choice; and independent per-resource minimum costs do not in general certify a feasible multi-resource continuation. We introduce FSPO, a feedback-state controller for budgeted LLM RL post-training that addresses these issues jointly. FSPO learns a policy-consistent risk-to-go model whose Bellman target follows the same frozen controller used for future decisions, together with a long-horizon utility model. Decision-conditioned trajectory calibration (DCTC) calibrates risk on cross-fitted trajectories generated by actions selected by provisional controllers. A Pareto resource continuation certificate (PRCC) admits an action only when a non-dominated cumulative reservation remains feasible over the residual horizon. Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO reaches 66.11% held-out and 59.43% OOD accuracy, compared with 64.47% and 57.03% for PB2, the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline. Three paired training seeds give gains of +2.42 and +3.19 percentage points over the contextual bandit on held-out and OOD evaluation. Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.
中文翻译
自适应的大语言模型强化学习后训练会在线调整多个训练参数，包括回滚温度、组大小、裁剪、KL正则化、验证器分配和更新预算。然而，三个耦合问题仍未解决：从行为轨迹训练的未来风险模型无需估计部署控制器引发的风险；在记录的状态-动作对上校准的评分在选择性动作后可能失准；独立的每资源最小成本通常无法保证多资源延续的可行性。我们提出FSPO，一种用于预算型大语言模型强化学习后训练的反馈状态控制器，联合解决这些问题。FSPO学习一个策略一致的风险剩余模型，其贝尔曼目标遵循未来决策所用的冻结控制器，并结合长期效用模型。决策条件轨迹校准（DCTC）在由临时控制器选择动作生成的交叉拟合轨迹上进行风险校准。帕累托资源延续证书（PRCC）仅在残余时间范围内非支配累积保留可行时才允许执行动作。在匹配的GRPO资源包络下，FSPO在保留测试集上达到66.11%准确率，在分布外测试上达到59.43%，优于最强的自适应基线PB2（分别为64.47%和57.03%）。三组配对训练种子在保留和分布外评估上分别比上下文老虎机提升2.42和3.19个百分点。在高行为-部署不匹配情况下，策略一致风险将选定决策的ECE从0.108降至0.053；DCTC在匹配接受条件下将其从0.039降至0.022；PRCC在18个动作目录中消除虚假可行准入（0.197→0.000）；启用全部三个组件使因子消融中的轨迹失败率从0.181降至0.083。
👤 Miaobo Hu, Shuhao Hu, Xiaobo Guo, Xin Wang, Bokun Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02808]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
ROUTEAUDIT: Interaction-Aware Identification for Budgeted Multi-Verifier Routing
ROUTEAUDIT：面向预算型多验证器路由的交互感知识别
Adaptive multi-verifier systems are commonly compared through endpoint quality-cost gaps, even when the verifier catalog, availability, accounting, information filtration, or scorer changes with the policy. We formulate verifier routing as a contract-conditioned identification problem. The contract records request support, verifier catalog, realized availability, resource accounting, online filtration, and post-trace scoring; a matched route contrast changes only the policy coordinate. ROUTEAUDIT adds three measurable objects to this contract. A contract lattice averages coordinate increments over every admissible bridge order and reports the resulting attribution together with its path sensitivity. A policy-independent response tape identifies paired sequential contrasts when adaptive policies reveal different observations. For incomplete matching, request-level bounds use whichever potential outcome remains observed and give a sharp finite-population interval. The protocol commits paid observations and ledger events before the oracle join and returns an attribution certificate for each comparison. On two held-out raw-tail caches, matched static SF+SA equals the cascade, assigning the apparent gains of 0.1797 and 0.1250 over full static to the verifier-set edge. On 1,319 held-out task requests, the learned and RLVR studies report quality 0.9522 and 0.9553 versus 0.9484 for matched static; the RLVR-static paired difference is +0.0068 with a request-paired interval $[0.0015,0.0122]$ and a training-seed-by-request hierarchical interval $[0.0006,0.0131]$. Controlled attribution recovery yields route mean absolute error 0.0011 and endpoint reconstruction error 0.0004. Factorial, bridge-order, and stochastic-provider studies evaluate the certificate interface; RLVR supplies a learned-policy stress test under the same identification contract.
中文翻译
自适应多验证器系统通常通过端点质量-成本差距进行比较，即使验证器目录、可用性、会计、信息过滤或评分器随策略变化。我们将验证器路由建模为合同条件下的识别问题。合同记录请求支持、验证器目录、实际可用性、资源会计、在线过滤和事后评分；匹配路径对比仅改变策略坐标。ROUTEAUDIT在此合同中添加了三个可度量对象。合同格网对所有可接受的桥接顺序的坐标增量取平均，并报告结果归因及其路径敏感性。策略无关响应带识别出当自适应策略揭示不同观察时的成对序列对比。对于不完全匹配，请求级边界采用仍可观察的潜在结果，并给出精确的有限总体区间。该协议在Oracle连接前承诺支付观察和账本事件，并为每次比较返回归因证书。在两个保留的原始尾部缓存上，匹配的静态SF+SA等于级联，将全静态的明显收益0.1797和0.1250归因于验证器集边缘。在1,319个保留任务请求上，学习和RLVR研究报告的质量分别为0.9522和0.9553，而匹配静态为0.9484；RLVR-静态配对差异为+0.0068，请求配对区间为[0.0015,0.0122]，训练种子-请求分层区间为[0.0006,0.0131]。受控归因恢复得到路径均方误差0.0011和端点重建误差0.0004。因子、桥接顺序和随机提供者研究评估了证书接口；RLVR在相同识别合同下提供了学习策略的压力测试。
👤 Miaobo Hu, Shuhao Hu, Xiaobo Guo, Xin Wang, Bokun Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02781]
agentic-rlagent-memory
OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation
RL之前先OPD：基于策略蒸馏的鲁棒性强化学习预热
Many useful language-model tasks cannot be evaluated by exact outcome verification. Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) addresses this issue by scoring open-ended responses against explicit criteria. However, because the reward is assigned after the complete response, the training signal does not directly identify which individual decisions contributed to the final score. We propose a two-stage training framework that uses rubrics first as privileged teacher context for dense token-level supervision, then as rewards for further RL. In the first stage, rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD), a student without access to the rubric matches a rubric-aware teacher's next-token distributions at student-generated prefixes. In the second stage, RL directly optimizes the rubric reward and improves beyond the observed distillation plateau. We evaluate the framework on health and science tasks using open-weight models. Across HealthBench, ResearchQA, and RubricHub Science, we compare post-training methods and vary the amount of SFT or RP-OPD training before RL, finding that our two-stage framework achieves the highest scores among the methods evaluated. RP-OPD + RL shows limited signs of reward hacking on RubricHub Science, whereas the SFT + RL baseline increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content. These findings support using rubrics to guide on-policy distillation before applying rubric-based RL.
中文翻译
许多有用的自然语言模型任务无法通过精确结果验证来评估。基于评分标准的强化学习（RL）通过将开放式响应与明确标准进行评分来解决这一问题。然而，由于奖励在完整响应生成后才分配，训练信号无法直接识别哪些个体决策贡献了最终得分。我们提出一种两阶段训练框架：首先将评分标准作为特权教师上下文，提供密集的token级监督；随后将其作为奖励用于进一步的强化学习。第一阶段为评分特权的策略内蒸馏（RP-OPD），学生在无评分访问权限的情况下，匹配一个具备评分意识的教师在其生成前缀上的下一个token分布。第二阶段直接优化评分奖励，并超越观察到的蒸馏平台。我们在健康和科学任务上使用开源模型评估该框架。在HealthBench、ResearchQA和RubricHub Science上，我们比较了后训练方法并改变SFT或RP-OPD训练量，发现我们的两阶段框架在所有评估方法中取得最高分数。RP-OPD + RL在RubricHub Science上表现出有限的奖励操纵迹象，而SFT + RL基线则越来越多地获得高奖励，尽管未提供所需内容。这些发现支持在应用基于评分的强化学习前，使用评分指导策略内蒸馏。
👤 Xinpeng Wang, Wei Shi, Yu-Chia Chen, Maria Zontak, Yun He et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Learning from Evolving Errors: Adaptive Iterative Repair for On-Policy Distillation
从演化错误中学习：面向策略内蒸馏的自适应迭代修复
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.
中文翻译
策略内自蒸馏（OPSD）为从学生自身策略采样的轨迹提供密集的token级反馈，相比强化学习的结果级奖励提供了更丰富的训练信号。这种反馈来自一个依赖于学生不可用的完整参考答案的教师。参考答案指定了目标，但未说明如何从学生的当前错误走向目标，从而产生解法条件的捷径风险。我们引入AIR-OPD，一种面向策略内蒸馏的自适应迭代修复框架，提供从错误到修复的监督。给定一个失败响应，引导生成器为当前错误合成修复指引。学生在该指引下采样策略内重试。如果重试仍不正确，生成器为新观察到的错误生成新的修复指引。每轮中，固定教师接收指引作为特权上下文，并监督学生在其最新失败响应中对齐错误的区域。结果感知阶段加权倾向于早期修复阶段，并奖励立即重试通过验证的阶段。我们在DAPO-Math-17K数据集上训练AIR-OPD，并在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25上进行评估，同时在MMLU-Pro和GPQA上进行分布外测试。我们考察了两种引导来源：当前学生策略的自引导和更大模型的外部引导。对于Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B，AIR-OPD在数学推理平均分上表现最佳，相较于最强基线最高提升3.6分，同时在分布外基准上保持了基础模型性能。
👤 Rui Li, Liyang He, Zheng Zhang, Zhenya Huang, Linbo Zhu et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

LEAP: Learning Efficient Action Proposals For LLM Agents
LEAP：学习高效动作提议以提升LLM智能体性能
LLM agents are known to be slow in rollouts. An agent completes a task one step at a time. At each step, it reasons and then chooses an action to execute. The next step and action cannot start until the previous one has finished. Speculative decoding accelerates the rollouts at the reason phase by drafting and verifying the inference tokens. Recent works have also started to apply similar ideas at the action phase. These works use off-the-shelf models, usually large, to draft action proposals for target model to verify. Large drafters match the target more often but take longer to propose, while small off-the-shelf models are fast but rarely make the same decision as the target. We ask a more general question: what determines the end-to-end speedup of action speculation? To answer it, we develop a latency framework for the speculative round. The framework compares what a round gains with what it costs. The gain depends on how well the drafter predicts the target and on how many steps the task can take before it ends. The cost comes from drafting, from waiting for target verification and from executing tools. Guided by the framework, we introduce LEAP (Learning Efficient Action Proposals) which keeps the drafter small and makes it accurate by training it on the target actions sequences. With a small 0.6B model, LEAP agrees with the target on most decisions and makes agents up to 60% faster in end-to-end wall clock time, with no systematic change in task success. Across various datasets, target models and draft models, the framework accounts for most of the measured speedups. We also show the draft model can be online trained with no prior trace collection and match the performance of offline training, making LEAP practical to deploy in the real world.
中文翻译
LLM智能体在执行过程中存在推理缓慢的问题。智能体逐步完成任务：每一步进行推理，然后选择并执行动作；下一步必须等待前一步完成后才能开始。推测解码通过草稿和验证推理标记加速推理阶段。最近工作也开始将类似思想应用于动作阶段，使用现成的大模型为目标模型草拟动作提议并由目标模型验证。大模型草稿器虽更常匹配目标，但提议耗时长；小模型草稿器虽快速，但很少做出与目标一致的决定。我们提出更普遍的问题：什么决定了动作推测的端到端加速？为此，我们构建了一个推测轮次的延迟框架，比较每轮带来的收益与成本。收益取决于草稿器对目标的预测能力以及任务在结束前可执行的步数；成本来自草稿生成、等待目标验证及工具执行。基于该框架，我们提出LEAP（学习高效动作提议），保持草稿器小型化并通过训练使其准确预测目标动作序列。使用仅0.6B的小模型，LEAP在多数决策上与目标一致，在端到端时钟时间上使智能体速度提升高达60%，且任务成功率无系统性变化。在多种数据集、目标模型与草稿模型组合中，该框架解释了大部分实测加速效果。我们还证明草稿模型可在无需预先收集轨迹的情况下在线训练，性能媲美离线训练，使LEAP在真实世界中具备可部署性。
👤 Zhen Xu, Qizheng Zhang, Gerry Wan, Shang Zhu, Ce Zhang 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

VERSE: Verified Self-Evolving Optimizer for Agent Harnesses
VERSE：用于智能体集成的验证自演化优化器
Harness evolution improves an LLM agent's prompts, tools, and workflow, while the optimizer's own tools and procedures often remain fixed. We study whether an optimizer can improve another agent more effectively by also improving how it diagnoses failures, develops edits, and tests their effects. Two observations guide our design. In a controlled study, optimizer self-evolution fails to improve performance without execution-based verification, but achieves the best result of that study when verification is available. Across five executors, self-evolving optimizers build their own tools for failure analysis, verification, training audits, and workflow control. Motivated by these findings, we introduce VERSE, a Verified Self-Evolving optimizer for agent harnesses. VERSE lets the optimizer test draft edits, replay failures, and perturb suspected steps before submission, while tracking fixes and regressions across rounds. Using this feedback, the optimizer revises both the executor harness and its own prompts, skills, tools, hooks, and notes, while the weights of the optimizer and executor models stay fixed. Under a shared protocol with disjoint training, validation, and test tasks, VERSE improves all four evaluated harness optimizers on held-out SWE-rebench tasks and newer out-of-distribution tasks in five languages. Its best validation-selected harness reaches 42.3% and 37.7% accuracy, respectively, against 39.2% and 29.3% for the strongest baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/wzekai/VERSE.
中文翻译
集成演化可改进LLM智能体的提示、工具与工作流，但优化器自身的工具与流程通常保持不变。我们研究优化器是否可通过同时改进其故障诊断、编辑制定与效果测试方式，更有效地优化其他智能体。两项观察指导我们的设计：在受控研究中，若无基于执行的验证，优化器自演化无法提升性能；但当验证可用时，其达到该研究的最佳结果。在五个执行器上，自演化优化器构建了用于故障分析、验证、训练审计与工作流控制的自有工具。基于这些发现，我们提出VERSE——一种用于智能体集成的验证自演化优化器。VERSE允许优化器测试草稿修改、重放故障、扰动可疑步骤后再提交，同时追踪各轮次中的修复与退化。利用此反馈，优化器不仅修订执行器集成，还同时优化其自身提示、技能、工具、钩子与笔记，而优化器与执行器模型权重保持不变。在共享协议下，使用分离的训练、验证与测试任务，VERSE在保留的SWE-rebench任务及五种语言的新分布外任务上均优于所有评估过的集成优化器。其最佳验证选中集成分别达到42.3%和37.7%的准确率，优于最强基线的39.2%和29.3%。代码已公开于 https://github.com/wzekai/VERSE。
👤 Zekai Wang, Yingqiang Ge, Zekun Wang, Hai Wang, Yuhui Xu et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Right Order, Wrong Scale: Auditing LLM Judges for Occupational AI Measurement
正确的顺序，错误的尺度：对LLM评判者在职业AI评估中的审计
LLM judges are increasingly used to assess whether AI outputs meet workplace requirements, but agreement on response rankings does not establish agreement on acceptance rates or occupational aggregates. We introduce O*NET-BENCH, an audit suite derived from an existing survey of 45,796 worker ratings, and evaluate 33 pre-existing judge configurations across six model families on 4,501 test ratings. Twenty-five configurations achieve tie-aware pair accuracy of at least 0.60, although a train-fitted response-only TF-IDF baseline nearly matches the strongest judge. Despite this ordering agreement, judges estimate that 3.0%-97.9% of responses are acceptable, compared with 61.1% for occupation-matched workers. In one fine-tuned lineage, changing from pointwise scoring to a bundled few-shot/listwise protocol improves response ordering while reducing agreement with worker means at the task and occupation levels; this reversal replicates on a task- and worker-disjoint validation split under prespecified criteria. Cross-validated calibration largely removes mean bias, but calibrated scores explain at most 8.5% of individual worker-rating variance. Prediction-assisted estimation yields at most small precision gains at the studied label budgets. These results show that ranking agreement alone is insufficient for occupational measurement. Judges should be validated against the acceptance rates and aggregates their scores will be used to estimate.
中文翻译
LLM评判者被越来越多地用于评估AI输出是否符合工作场所要求，但响应排序的一致性并不能保证接受率或职业总体指标的一致性。我们提出了O*NET-BENCH，一个基于45,796名工人评分调查的审计套件，并在4,501个测试评分上评估了六个模型族中的33种现有评判配置。25种配置达到了至少0.60的考虑平局的成对准确率，尽管仅使用响应的TF-IDF基线（经过训练拟合）几乎与最强的评判者相当。尽管存在排序一致性，评判者估计可接受响应的比例在3.0%至97.9%之间，而与职业匹配的工人则为61.1%。在一个微调谱系中，从点对点评分改为捆绑式少量示例/列表式协议改善了响应排序，但降低了与工人平均值在任务和职业层面的一致性；这一反转在预设标准下的任务和工人不重叠验证划分中重现。交叉验证校准基本消除了均值偏差，但校准后的分数最多解释8.5%的个体工人评分方差。预测辅助估计在所研究的标签预算下仅带来微小的精度提升。这些结果表明，仅靠排序一致性不足以进行职业测量。评判者应针对其评分将被用来估算的接受率和总体指标进行验证。

Capability Scaling-Down Laws for LLM Compression
大语言模型压缩的能力缩放-下行定律
LLM compression reduces inference costs and memory requirements, but selecting a method and configuration remains largely empirical because comparable resource reductions can produce different capability losses. We systematically investigate capability scaling-down laws for LLM compression across pruning, quantization, and distillation. Our framework measures capability loss in mathematics, code generation, and question answering, and relates these measurements to model size, training stage, compression settings, data availability, and training exposure. We develop simple predictive relations and evaluate their accuracy, measurement efficiency, and generalization to unseen configurations and model states. Sharing the density response across pruning levels halves the configuration measurements needed to fit a pruning predictor: on new Pythia states, on pre-registered OLMo-2 test states and under Wanda pruning, the compact relation matches a regression fitted with all measurements on math and code to within 0.020 nats per token, with coefficients refitted for each setting. Controlled distillation experiments show that the cost of heavy data reuse recurs across question-answering distributions, while the net benefit depends on the evaluation distribution. We further evaluate the decision value of these predictions by comparing numerical selection with configuration medians and fixed method priorities. Independent evaluations across two model families show that selection captures most of the available cross-method benefit for question answering within the tested candidate sets, where a fixed method priority attains the same regret, with smaller opportunities for mathematics and code. These results clarify the predictive scope of capability scaling-down laws and their use in compression method selection. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/scaling_down_law.
中文翻译
大语言模型压缩可以降低推理成本和内存需求，但选择方法和配置仍主要依赖经验，因为相似的资源缩减可能带来不同的能力损失。我们系统地研究了剪枝、量化和蒸馏三种方法在大语言模型压缩中的能力缩放-下行定律。我们的框架衡量数学、代码生成和问答任务中的能力损失，并将其与模型大小、训练阶段、压缩设置、数据可用性和训练暴露程度相关联。我们建立了简单的预测关系，并评估其准确性、测量效率以及对未见配置和模型状态的泛化能力。在不同剪枝水平间共享密度响应，使拟合剪枝预测器所需的配置测量数量减半：在新的Pythia状态、预先注册的OLMo-2测试状态以及Wanda剪枝下，紧凑关系在数学和代码任务上的表现与使用全部测量数据拟合的回归相比误差不超过0.020纳特/词元，且每个设置系数重新拟合。受控蒸馏实验表明，大量数据重复使用在问答分布中产生持续成本，而净收益取决于评估分布。我们进一步通过比较数值选择与配置中位数和固定方法优先级来评估这些预测的决策价值。在两个模型族的独立评估中，选择方法捕获了测试候选集中问答任务的大部分跨方法优势，而固定方法优先级达到相同遗憾值；在数学和代码任务中机会较小。这些结果明确了能力缩放-下行定律的预测范围及其在压缩方法选择中的应用。代码已公开：https://github.com/LabRAI/scaling_down_law。
👤 Xueqi Cheng, Liang Wu, Kelly Wan, Liangjie Hong, Yushun Dong 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating and Improving the Robustness of Large Language Models to Input Sequence Variations
评估并提升大语言模型对输入序列变化的鲁棒性
Large language models (LLMs) in production systems face prompt injections, trojans (backdoors), and manipulation of automatic quality metrics. This thesis develops models, methods, and algorithms for evaluating and improving LLM robustness to adversarial input sequence variations. We propose R_stab(f), a generative robustness metric based on the Jensen-Shannon divergence between per-step output distributions under small input perturbations. For localized attacks we prove V(h) <= 1 - R_class(h), where R_class(h) is the probability that a decision operator h keeps its decision under small perturbations. For non-localized attacks we propose a calibrated empirical model. For LLM-as-a-Judge systems we develop ASA, an adaptive evolutionary black-box attack that reaches an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 73.8%, with transfer between open models up to 62.6%. On Trojan Detection Challenge 2023 data (Pythia-1.4B), surrogate triggers reach REASR ~0.99 while recall of the true triggers is ~0.17 against a baseline of ~0.14. On SaTML CTF 2024 we systematize four classes of bypasses of multi-layer defenses, which reduce the ASR from 90% to 15-25%. Committees of 5-7 heterogeneous models reduce the ASR for Gemma-3-4B by 47-55 percentage points, to 19.3% with 7 models. For agentic systems based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), we propose AttestMCP, which attests tool calls with HMAC-protected packets at under 0.1 ms per call, and the Commit Boundary isolation pattern. On the MCPBench benchmark of 847 scenarios they reduce the average ASR from 53.7% to 12.4%. The methods are implemented in the JudgeGuard and TrojanArmor software suites and the MCPSec module.
中文翻译
生产系统中的大语言模型面临提示注入、木马攻击（后门）以及自动质量度量的操纵。本论文开发了模型、方法和算法，用于评估和提升LLM对对抗性输入序列变化的鲁棒性。我们提出R_stab(f)，一种基于小输入扰动下每步输出分布的Jensen-Shannon散度的生成鲁棒性度量。针对局部攻击，我们证明V(h) ≤ 1 - R_class(h)，其中R_class(h)是决策算子h在小扰动下保持决策的概率。针对非局部攻击，我们提出一种校准的经验模型。针对LLM作为评判者的系统，我们开发了ASA，一种自适应进化黑盒攻击，在开放模型间转移攻击成功率最高达62.6%，攻击成功率达73.8%。在2023年木马检测挑战赛数据（Pythia-1.4B）上，代理触发器达到REASR ~0.99，而真实触发器召回率仅为~0.17，优于基线~0.14。在SaTML CTF 2024中，我们系统化分析了四类绕过多层防御的方法，使攻击成功率从90%降至15%-25%。由5-7个异构模型组成的委员会可使Gemma-3-4B的攻击成功率降低47-55个百分点，降至19.3%（7个模型时）。针对基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的智能体系统，我们提出AttestMCP，通过HMAC保护的数据包在每次调用耗时低于0.1毫秒内验证工具调用，并采用提交边界隔离模式。在包含847个场景的MCPBench基准上，平均攻击成功率从53.7%降至12.4%。这些方法已集成于JudgeGuard和TrojanArmor软件套件及MCPSec模块中。

Trained Agentic Context Management
训练有素的智能体上下文管理
We study long context language models. Instead of training long context natively, or designing a long context harness, we train a model over the simplest possible harness: a tool to call itself with any specified prompt and a tool to read tokens in a range from the input context. We finetune Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on a diverse synthetic dataset using this harness. With only 8,000 tokens of context, our small model is as strong as GPT-5.4 with 1M tokens of context on the OOLONG-synth benchmark when document length exceeds 40K tokens.
中文翻译
我们研究长上下文语言模型。不同于原生训练长上下文或设计复杂的长上下文机制，我们使用最简单的机制对模型进行训练：一个可调用自身并指定任意提示的工具，以及一个可读取输入上下文中指定范围token的工具。我们在多样化合成数据集上对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B进行微调，仅使用8,000个token的上下文，我们的小型模型在文档长度超过40K token时，其性能与拥有100万token上下文的GPT-5.4在OOLONG-synth基准上相当。

Hesitation Has a Geometry: Entropy-Trained Hyperbolic Probes for Sparse Activation Steering
犹豫具有几何性：基于熵训练的双曲探测器用于稀疏激活引导
When a large language model solves a mathematical problem, its reasoning is largely hierarchical, and the solution often branches at a few tokens where the next-token entropy is high. Such tree-like structure embeds in hyperbolic space with far lower distortion than in Euclidean space. Activation steering, however, usually edits the hidden states of a pretrained model by adding one fixed Euclidean vector at every token, even though most tokens of a solution are already determined by the context. We propose Hyperbolic Entropy Steering (HEST), which embeds the hidden states in the Poincaré ball with a lightweight probe whose only label is the model's own next-token entropy. Where this entropy exceeds a threshold, HEST moves the embedded state along the geodesic of steepest descent of a readout of the probe and maps the change back to the hidden state. For the Busemann readout of a learned ideal point, we prove that a step of fixed length lowers it by the same amount at every state. On three instruction-tuned models from the Qwen2.5-Math and Llama-3.1 families, HEST with the Busemann readout improves greedy accuracy on MATH-500 and GSM8K in five of six settings, by up to 1.8 points, whereas a contrastive steering vector added at every token lowers accuracy. With a Euclidean probe trained in the same way, this gain disappears on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct. The gains are largest on problems where the model hesitates often, and accuracy on the remaining problems is almost unchanged.
中文翻译
当大型语言模型解决数学问题时，其推理过程具有明显的层次结构，解决方案通常在少数几个词元处分叉，此时下一个词元的熵值较高。这种树状结构在双曲空间中的嵌入畸变远小于欧几里得空间。然而，现有的激活引导方法通常在每个词元处添加一个固定的欧几里得向量来修改预训练模型的隐藏状态，尽管大多数词元的解已经由上下文决定。我们提出双曲熵引导（HEST），将隐藏状态嵌入庞加莱球中，使用仅以模型自身下一个词元熵为标签的轻量级探测器。当该熵超过阈值时，HEST沿着探测器读出函数梯度下降方向的测地线移动嵌入状态，并将变化映射回隐藏状态。对于学习的理想点的布塞曼读出，我们证明固定长度的步长在所有状态下均降低相同量。在Qwen2.5-Math和Llama-3.1系列的三个指令微调模型上，使用布塞曼读出的HEST在MATH-500和GSM8K上的贪婪准确率在六种设置中有五种提升，最高达1.8分；而每词元添加对比引导向量则降低了准确率。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct上，使用相同方式训练的欧几里得探测器无法获得此增益。收益最大的是模型频繁犹豫的问题，其余问题的准确率几乎不变。
👤 Zeyong Zhang, Tung Sum Thomas Kwok, Tengfei Ma, Mengjia Xu 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02361]

SEDIMA: Cross-Run Hierarchical Insight Memory for Evolutionary Search Agents
SEDIMA：进化搜索智能体的跨运行层次洞察记忆
Large language model (LLM)-driven evolutionary search is a powerful paradigm for automated program and algorithm discovery, yet existing systems are largely memoryless: each run explores from scratch, so agents repeatedly rediscover the same improvements and re-encounter the same dead ends. We introduce SEDIMA, a persistent hierarchical insight memory for evolutionary search agents. SEDIMA distills raw traces into natural-language insights, clusters them by semantic similarity using attention-weighted centroids, and retrieves relevant guidance to condition future mutations, accumulating transferable knowledge across runs and problems rather than within a single trajectory. As a drop-in module that leaves the search operators unmodified, SEDIMA improves average final performance by 5.5% on AlgoTune and 6.6% on ALE-Bench LITE under a fixed budget of 100 evaluated candidates. Under OpenEvolve, SEDIMA requires 32.3% fewer iterations on average to reach baseline-best performance across the five evaluated backbones.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）的进化搜索是自动化程序与算法发现的强大范式，但现有系统大多缺乏记忆：每次运行都从零开始探索，导致智能体反复重新发现相同的改进并遭遇相同的死胡同。我们引入SEDIMA，一种面向进化搜索智能体的持久化层次洞察记忆。SEDIMA将原始轨迹提炼为自然语言洞察，利用注意力加权中心点对它们按语义相似性聚类，并检索相关指导以调节未来的突变，从而在不同运行和问题间积累可迁移的知识，而非局限于单一轨迹。作为无需修改搜索算子的即插即用模块，SEDIMA在固定100个候选评估预算下，使AlgoTune和ALE-Bench LITE的平均最终性能分别提升5.5%和6.6%。在OpenEvolve上，SEDIMA平均减少32.3%的迭代次数即可达到基准最优性能，覆盖五个评估骨干网络。
👤 Amirhossein Abaskohi, Mahdi Mostajabdaveh, Zirui Zhou 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02359]
agentic-rl
Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation
字典序多目标在线策略蒸馏
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually optimizes answer correctness, yet useful language-model behavior also requires high-quality reasoning and concise responses. Existing multi-reward post-training methods typically scalarize rewards or combine specialists without explicitly protecting a reward priority order. This is problematic when trade-offs are asymmetric: conciseness, for example, should not improve at the cost of correctness. We introduce Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD), a multi-teacher method for integrating reward-specialized policies under explicit priorities. For each student rollout, LMOPD selects the specialist for the first objective whose gate detects a deficiency, then locally projects its centered log-policy correction to remove components that oppose higher-priority specialists. We evaluate 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts transformer models in two- and four-expert settings on three math benchmarks, measuring retained specialist gains. With two experts, LMOPD's point estimates fully retain the accuracy and reasoning-quality gains while acquiring $46.9\%$ of the conciseness gain. With four experts, it retains $\approx90\%$ of both the accuracy gain and reasoning-correctness gain, compared to only $\approx57\%$ by the next best evaluated baseline. Matched four-expertablations show that lexicographic routing outperforms random routing and that projection further strengthens both top-priority capabilities. Across both scales, LMOPD preserves the highest-priority capabilities more effectively than the existing baselines we evaluate, demonstrating the value of explicit priorities for specialist integration.
中文翻译
从可验证奖励中进行强化学习（RLVR）通常优化答案正确性，但高质量的语言模型行为还要求优质推理过程和简洁回应。现有多种奖励后训练方法通常将奖励标量合并或组合专家模型，却未显式保护奖励优先级顺序。当权衡不对称时，这会带来问题：例如，简洁性不应以牺牲正确性为代价。我们提出字典序多目标在线策略蒸馏（LMOPD），一种在显式优先级下整合奖励专业化策略的多教师方法。对于每个学生轨迹，LMOPD选择首个目标的专家，其门控机制检测到缺陷后，局部投影其中心化日志策略修正项，移除与更高优先级专家相悖的成分。我们在两个和四个专家设置下，对三个数学基准测试中的30B-A3B专家混合Transformer模型进行评估，测量保留的专家增益。在双专家设置下，LMOPD完全保留准确率和推理质量增益，同时获得46.9%的简洁性增益；在四专家设置下，它保留约90%的准确率增益和推理正确性增益，而次优基线仅保留约57%。匹配的四专家消融实验表明，字典序路由优于随机路由，且投影进一步增强了高优先级能力。在两种规模下，LMOPD比所评估的现有基线更有效地保持高优先级能力，证明了显式优先级在专家集成中的价值。
👤 Doseok Jang, Jon Ander Campos, Youran Qi 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02267]

Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses
快速模型，缓慢证据：对LLM智能体中系统1决策模型的配对自审计评估
Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.
中文翻译
智能体工作流在每个任务中需做出大量小型、类型化的决策，例如调用哪个模型、使用哪个工具、检索文本是否相关、输入是否存在注入攻击。系统1决策模型通过单次前向传播和类别概率来回答这些问题，相比大语言模型调用有望显著降低成本和延迟。我们对一个开源权重模型（Laya）和一个托管模型（Jev）在11个基于18个公开数据源构建的智能体决策点上进行了配对评估：包含7,283个基础案例及6,640个鲁棒性变体，采用字节完全相同的输入、配对测试以及跨硬件和跨日可复现性检查。结果显示，Jev在11个决策点中的9个上显著更准确（提升10.8至46.0个百分点）。两个模型在零样本模型路由任务中均未超越随机水平，且在RAG相关性门控任务上表现持平。Laya在选项顺序反转时会改变30%的答案，并在候选项数量多或相似时性能急剧下降（50个最近邻工具时准确率为31%，而Jev在唯一正确工具的情况下准确率达98%）。我们还对自身流水线进行了审计，发现三个分析错误和一个设计混淆导致了主要部署结论的夸大：遗漏预筛选成本（报告节省23.9%，实际仅4.3%）、将门控准确率误报为端到端质量（58% vs. 98%）、样本内阈值设定（目标5%，最高达17%的外部遗漏），以及“通道效应”在通道原生内容下消失的假象。另外两个疑似混淆因素未改变结论。所有案例、原始输出和分析代码已公开于https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval。
👤 Jiawei Li 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02233]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Budgeted Cache Repair for Cross-Context KV-Cache Reuse
预算约束的缓存修复用于跨上下文KV缓存重用
Cross-context KV-cache reuse predicts a shared segment's keys and values under a new prefix instead of recomputing them, and has been reported to do so without quality loss. We find otherwise, and identify two problems. (1) A hidden cost: on MMLU and GSM8K, reuse costs substantial accuracy. (2) A decision at the wrong unit: no rule for deciding whether to reuse a cache removes that cost. What does help is choosing which parts of the cache to recompute, and the value of choosing well falls as the unit of choice grows: informed selection removes 49.5% of the cache error beyond chance at single rows (one token's keys and values), 10.6% at 64-token chunks, and nothing at the level of whole calls. Budgeted Cache Repair (BCR) acts at the unit where selection still pays. It drafts two tokens from the assembled cache, ranks cache rows by the attention those tokens pay them, and recomputes a fixed budget of rows exactly, in one of three layouts. The cost is paid rather than predicted away, and the draft that fails as a gate succeeds as a selector. BCR restores GSM8K to dense-prefill accuracy while still serving most calls from cache, and its best layout outperforms every reuse baseline's mean in the reference grid. The draft also beats a coin-flip selector at the same budget - a control prior evaluations lack.
中文翻译
跨上下文KV缓存重用通过在新前缀下预测共享段的键和值，而非重新计算，已被报道可实现无质量损失的重用。但我们发现实际情况并非如此，并识别出两个问题：(1) 隐藏成本：在MMLU和GSM8K基准上，重用导致显著的准确性下降；(2) 决策单位错误：缺乏决定是否重用缓存的规则，使得该成本无法避免。真正有效的是选择哪些缓存部分进行重新计算，且随着选择单位增大，选择的价值迅速下降：在单行级别（一个token的键值对）可消除49.5%的缓存误差（超出随机水平），在64-token块级别为10.6%，而在整个调用级别则无效。预算约束缓存修复（BCR）在仍具价值的选择单位上操作。它从组装后的缓存中抽取两个token，根据这些token对缓存行的注意力权重进行排序，并以三种布局之一精确重新计算固定预算的缓存行。成本被实际支付而非预测规避，而原本作为门控失败的草稿反而成为有效的选择器。BCR在保持大多数请求从缓存服务的同时，将GSM8K性能恢复至密集预填充水平，其最优布局在参考网格中优于所有重用基线的平均表现。此外，该草稿在相同预算下也优于随机选择器——这是此前评估所缺乏的对照实验。
👤 Haeyong Kang, Chang D. Yoo 📅 2026-09-27 🔗 arXiv

[2610.03625]
FALCON: A Model and Dataset Agnostic Framework for Synthetic Data Generation for NL2SQL Pairs
FALCON：一种模型与数据集无关的NL2SQL合成数据生成框架
Relational databases are among the most widely deployed forms of structured knowledge, and natural language access to them requires grounding language onto schema entities and relations while handling the ambiguity inherent in how people phrase requests. Existing synthetic NL-to-SQL data generation methods largely ignore this ambiguity and produce oversimplified queries that fail to prepare models for the complexity of real-world structured knowledge access. We present FALCON, a framework that generates realistic, ambiguity-aware NL-to-SQL data matching the complexity of challenging real-world benchmarks, at low cost using compact open models. Our approach combines reserved-word SQL seeding and persona-based prompting to generate structurally complex queries, while alignment-based filtering preserves difficulty by distinguishing genuinely incorrect examples from complex but valid queries. Human evaluation confirms consistent high quality across model sizes, and our generated data exceeds existing benchmarks in both SQL complexity and natural language richness. Difficulty-stratified analysis shows models trained on FALCON data increasingly outperform baseline-trained models as query complexity increases, validating our pipeline's success in generating challenging training data. When combined with a small proportion of existing benchmark data, mixed training recovers performance on simpler queries while preserving these advantages on complex ones. The model- and database-agnostic design enables organizations to generate high-complexity NL-to-SQL training data locally without external APIs.
中文翻译
关系数据库是应用最广泛的结构化知识形式之一，对它们进行自然语言访问需要将语言与模式实体和关系对齐，同时处理人们表达请求时固有的模糊性。现有的合成NL-to-SQL数据生成方法大多忽略这种模糊性，生成过于简化的查询，无法让模型为真实世界结构化知识访问的复杂性做好准备。我们提出FALCON，一种利用小型开源模型以低成本生成符合挑战性真实世界基准复杂度的逼真、具有模糊意识的NL-to-SQL数据的框架。我们的方法结合保留字SQL种子和基于角色的提示生成结构复杂的查询，而基于对齐的过滤机制通过区分真正错误的示例与复杂但有效的查询来保持难度。人工评估证实了不同模型规模下的一致高质量，且生成的数据在SQL复杂性和自然语言丰富性方面均超过现有基准。按难度分层分析显示，使用FALCON数据训练的模型在查询复杂性增加时，性能逐渐超越基线训练模型，验证了我们流水线在生成挑战性训练数据方面的成功。当与少量现有基准数据混合训练时，混合训练在保持复杂查询优势的同时恢复了对简单查询的性能。该模型和数据库无关的设计使组织能够在本地生成高复杂度的NL-to-SQL训练数据，无需外部API。
👤 Darian Lee, Shannon Rumsey, Jack St. Clair, Xinyi Tang, Aditya Bansal et al. 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Writerslogic at the CLEF 2026 SimpleText Track: Multi-Candidate LLM Simplification and Stacked Complexity Spotting
Writerslogic在CLEF 2026 SimpleText赛道的表现：多候选LLM简化与堆叠复杂度识别
We describe the Writerslogic team's participation in the CLEF 2026 SimpleText shared task, addressing Task 1 (text simplification) and Task 2 (complexity spotting). For Task 1, we develop a multi-candidate generation pipeline using GPT-4o-mini that produces five simplification candidates per sentence at varying temperatures, then selects the best candidate using a reference-free scoring heuristic that rewards compression, source word retention, Cochrane Plain Language Summary vocabulary usage, and lexical simplicity. On Task 1.1 (sentence-level simplification), our Claude Sonnet 4 submission achieves SARI 47.43 and BLEU 14.21, the top-ranked sentence-level system (3rd on the combined Task 1 leaderboard, behind two document-level submissions). For Task 2, we fine-tune a DeBERTa-v3-large NLI model on 350K labeled (source, sentence) pairs, framing hallucination detection as natural language inference. The model reads the most relevant source sentence as premise and the candidate as hypothesis, directly learning to distinguish grounded from hallucinated content. On Task 2.1 (binary overgeneration identification), our fine-tuned DeBERTa system achieves 0.8081 document-level macro F1 (0.8085 in our best ensemble), the top-ranked entry within the identification track and 2nd among teams overall, behind AIIR Lab (0.8197). On Task 2.2 (multi-class error classification), our best submission reaches 0.804 multiclass accuracy, ranking 2nd among unique teams behind AIIR Lab (0.827). We evaluate both tasks on English and multilingual biomedical text from Cochrane systematic reviews.
中文翻译
我们描述了Writerslogic团队参与CLEF 2026 SimpleText共享任务的情况，涵盖任务1（文本简化）和任务2（复杂度识别）。对于任务1，我们开发了一个使用GPT-4o-mini的多候选生成流水线，每句生成五个不同温度下的简化候选，然后通过无参考评分启发式方法选择最佳候选，该方法奖励压缩程度、源词保留、Cochrane通俗语言摘要词汇使用以及词汇简洁性。在任务1.1（句子级简化）中，我们的Claude Sonnet 4提交在SARI上得分为47.43，在BLEU上得分为14.21，成为句子级系统中的最高排名（在综合任务1排行榜中位列第三，仅次于两个文档级提交）。对于任务2，我们对35万条标注的（源文本，句子）对微调了一个DeBERTa-v3-large NLI模型，将幻觉检测建模为自然语言推理问题。模型将最相关的源句作为前提，候选句作为假设，直接学习区分有依据内容与幻觉内容。在任务2.1（二元过度生成识别）中，我们微调的DeBERTa系统在文档级宏F1上达到0.8081（最佳集成达0.8085），在识别赛道中排名第一，在所有团队中位列第二（仅次于AIIR Lab的0.8197）。在任务2.2（多类别错误分类）中，我们最佳提交达到0.804的多类别准确率，在独立团队中排名第二（仅次于AIIR Lab的0.827）。我们在英语及来自Cochrane系统综述的多语种生物医学文本上评估了这两个任务。
👤 David L. Condrey 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03565]
Writerslogic at PAN 2026: Process over Content for Robust Detection under Domain Shift
Writerslogic在PAN 2026中的表现：过程优于内容以应对领域偏移的鲁棒检测
We describe the Writerslogic systems for three PAN at CLEF 2026 shared tasks (Reasoning Trajectory Detection, Voight-Kampff Generative AI Detection, and Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis), unified by a shared analytical framework: feature robustness under distribution shift is governed by support overlap between training and test distributions, not by training-set effect size. This yields a taxonomy (domain-anchored, domain-portable, domain-invariant) that explains why generator-specific features die under domain shift while vocabulary fingerprints (hapax ratio, Yule's K, Heaps' exponent), compression measures, and character n-grams survive. On Reasoning Trajectory Detection, where training was entirely mathematics and 84 percent of test was unseen domains, the framework guided system design to 1st place in source detection (0.85 macro F1 via Opus-Sonnet agreement) and 3rd place in safety classification (0.66 macro F1 via query-refusal decomposition). For Voight-Kampff, we built a calibrated ensemble of DeBERTa-v2 (ONNX), multi-seed LightGBM with 44 domain-portable stylometric features, and SVM on n-gram TF-IDF, combined via learned stacking with isotonic calibration; the best configuration achieved 0.891 on the PAN 2026 test set with balanced sub-metrics (0.853 to 0.902 across all evaluation dimensions). For Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis, we describe a system fusing spectral clustering over character n-gram similarity graphs, normalized compression distance for local boundary detection, and SmolLM-135M perplexity for neural change-point detection; a platform mix-up meant our run never reached the official evaluation, so we report the design and its a priori predictions. Across all three tasks, features measuring generation process properties are designed to outperform features measuring generated content properties under domain shift.
中文翻译
我们描述了Writerslogic在CLEF 2026 PAN共享任务中的系统表现，包括推理轨迹检测、Voight-Kampff生成式AI检测和多作者写作风格分析。这三个任务共享一个统一的分析框架：特征在分布偏移下的鲁棒性由训练与测试分布之间的支持重叠决定，而非训练集效应大小。这引出了一个分类体系（领域锚定、领域可迁移、领域不变），解释了为何生成器特定特征在领域偏移下失效，而词汇指纹（孤立词比率、Yule's K、Heaps指数）、压缩度量和字符n-gram特征则能存活。在推理轨迹检测任务中，训练数据完全来自数学领域，而84%的测试数据来自未见领域，该框架指导系统设计，使源检测获得第一名（Opus-Sonnet一致率下宏F1为0.85），安全分类获得第三名（查询拒绝分解下宏F1为0.66）。在Voight-Kampff任务中，我们构建了一个校准集成系统，包含DeBERTa-v2（ONNX）、44个领域可迁移风格特征的多种子LightGBM以及基于n-gram TF-IDF的SVM，通过学习堆叠和等距校准组合；最佳配置在PAN 2026测试集上取得0.891的得分，各评估维度子指标平衡（0.853至0.902）。在多作者写作风格分析任务中，我们描述了一个融合谱聚类（基于字符n-gram相似图）、归一化压缩距离（用于局部边界检测）和SmolLM-135M困惑度（用于神经变化点检测）的系统；由于平台配置错误，我们的运行未进入正式评估，因此仅报告设计及其先验预测。在所有三项任务中，旨在衡量生成过程属性的特征被设计为在领域偏移下优于衡量生成内容属性的特征。

[2610.03324]
To Jev or Not? Evaluating the Accuracy and Efficiency of Structured Decision Models for Hate-Speech Moderation
是否采用Jev？评估结构化决策模型在仇恨言论审核中的准确性和效率
The scale of online content makes hate-speech moderation challenging, while Large Language Models (LLMs) enable harmful material to be produced and adapted more easily. Moderation therefore requires efficient classifiers that can accommodate different definitions of hate speech. Recent structured decision models accept natural-language criteria and select among specified answers, raising the question of whether they can meet these requirements without task-specific training. We present HATEDECIDE, an evaluation of six decision-model configurations on four hate-speech datasets against specialized moderation, zero-shot, commercial, and supervised baselines. We examine whether supplying a dataset's definition, or decomposing it into multiple questions, improves classification, and we measure their latency and cost. We find that commercial LLMs significantly outperform all decision models on only one dataset. Supplying definitions changes up to 28\% of predictions without consistently improving classification, and decomposition significantly improves performance in only 20\% of the comparisons. On a diagnostic set of test cases, the best hosted decision model comes within 1.6 macro-F1 points of the best commercial LLM at approximately 97\% lower inference cost. These results identify opportunities for inexpensive moderation, while showing that explicit criteria and additional questions do not reliably improve classification.
中文翻译
在线内容的规模使得仇恨言论审核极具挑战性，而大型语言模型（LLMs）则使有害内容的生成和修改变得更加容易。因此，审核需要高效的分类器，能够适应不同定义的仇恨言论。最近的结构化决策模型接受自然语言标准并从指定答案中进行选择，这引发了它们是否能在无需特定任务训练的情况下满足这些要求的问题。我们提出了HATEDECIDE，对六种决策模型配置在四个仇恨言论数据集上进行了评估，对比了专门的审核、零样本、商业和监督基线。我们研究了提供数据集定义或将其分解为多个问题是否能提升分类性能，并测量了其延迟和成本。结果发现，商业LLM仅在一个数据集上显著优于所有决策模型。提供定义最多可改变28%的预测结果，但并未一致提升分类效果；分解仅在20%的比较中显著提升性能。在诊断测试集上，表现最佳的托管决策模型在约97%更低的推理成本下，与最佳商业LLM的宏F1得分差距仅为1.6分。这些结果揭示了低成本审核的机会，同时表明显式标准和额外问题并不能可靠地改善分类。
👤 Demetris Paschalides, George Pallis, Marios D. Dikaiakos 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03268]
Shrome at Touché: Soft-Vote Ensembling and Counter-Causal Augmentation for Causality Extraction
Touché 2026：软投票集成与反因果增强用于因果关系抽取
Touché 2026 extends causality extraction to counter-causal claims: news sentences whose surface form appears causal but whose meaning denies the causation, as in "It is falsely believed that X caused Y." A system that relies on surface cues such as "caused" or "led to" will accept such a sentence as causal and give it the wrong polarity. On the Countercausal News Corpus (CCNC), the task has three subtasks: deciding whether a sentence is causal (detection), locating its cause and effect spans (extraction), and labeling its polarity as procausal, counter-causal, or uncausal. We build one model per subtask. Detection is a fine-tuned classifier with a single cross-task rule that uses the extracted spans to remove false positives. For extraction, we ensemble three RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF taggers by averaging their token-level scores before decoding, rather than voting on the spans each tagger produces. For polarity, where labeled counter-causal examples are scarcest, we add training sentences generated by a large language model prompted with nine patterns of counter-causal expression adapted from Hagen et al., keeping only those that pass automatic structural checks. On the held-out CCNC test set, the system reaches F1 0.869 on detection and macro-F1 0.817 on polarity, and in the organizers' final causal-only evaluation of extraction it scores granularity-adjusted F1 0.728, the highest extraction score among all submissions including the organizers' baseline. The development split is used only for component selection and the ablations reported in the paper.
中文翻译
Touché 2026将因果关系抽取扩展至反因果陈述：表面形式看似因果，但实际含义否定因果关系的新闻句子，例如“人们错误地认为X导致了Y”。依赖于“导致”或“引起”等表面线索的系统会错误地将此类句子识别为因果，并赋予错误极性。在反因果新闻语料库（CCNC）上，该任务包含三个子任务：判断句子是否为因果（检测）、定位其原因和结果片段（抽取），以及标注其极性为正因果、反因果或无因果。我们为每个子任务构建一个模型。检测使用经过微调的分类器，结合单一跨任务规则，利用提取的片段来消除误报。对于抽取，我们通过平均三个RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF标记器的token级分数进行解码，而非对各标记器产生的片段进行投票。对于极性任务，由于标注的反因果样本最少，我们使用大语言模型根据Hagen等人提出的九种反因果表达模式生成训练句，仅保留通过自动结构检查的句子。在预留的CCNC测试集上，系统在检测任务上达到F1 0.869，在极性任务上达到宏F1 0.817；在组织者最终的纯因果评估中，抽取任务得分为粒度调整后的F1 0.728，是所有提交方案（包括组织者基线）中的最高分。开发集仅用于组件选择及文中报告的消融实验。

[2610.03110]
Ontological Instability and Statistical Amplification: The Paradox of "Humanizing" LLM-Generated Text
本体不稳定性与统计放大：'人性化'大语言模型生成文本的悖论
Supervised AI-text detectors report high benchmark accuracy, but it is not clear what their decisions are based on. We analyze a RoBERTa-based detector under semantic, structural, and tokenizer-level perturbations, using the M4 dataset (N = 10,000) and controlled generations (N = 300). When Mistral-7B-Instruct was asked to make machine text sound more human, Verb Diversity rose from 0.77 to 0.92 and the outputs became easier to detect. Detection scores appear to track statistical complexity, which also leads to a 76.3% false-positive rate on formal human writing. As a control, we evaluate event-based Latent Space detection. Paraphrasing changed 87% of its event sequences (Jaccard = 0.067), and homoglyphs altered 70% of the extracted verbs even though extraction still ran (Jaccard = 0.30). Its best domain AUC was 0.577. RoBERTa's robustness seems specific to the features it uses, and structural abstraction did not make detection more robust.
中文翻译
监督式AI文本检测器报告了高基准准确率，但其决策依据尚不明确。我们分析了一个基于RoBERTa的检测器，在语义、结构和分词器级别扰动下的表现，使用M4数据集（N=10,000）和受控生成样本（N=300）。当要求Mistral-7B-Instruct使机器生成文本更像人类时，动词多样性从0.77上升至0.92，输出反而更容易被检测。检测分数似乎追踪统计复杂性，这也导致正式人类写作中76.3%的误报率。作为对照，我们评估了基于事件的潜在空间检测方法。改写使87%的事件序列发生变化（Jaccard = 0.067），同形异义字符改变了70%提取出的动词，尽管提取仍能运行（Jaccard = 0.30）。其最佳领域AUC为0.577。RoBERTa的鲁棒性似乎仅针对其使用的特定特征，而结构抽象并未提升检测鲁棒性。

[2610.03078]
An automated pipeline for standardised speech-unit annotation in spontaneous dialogue
自发对话中标准化语音单元标注的自动化流程
Quantifying conversational dynamics requires reliable identification of interactional units and their temporal boundaries, but speech activity alone does not distinguish conversational turns from listener feedback or within-turn pauses. We present an automated pipeline for extracting turns and backchannels from separate-channel recordings of spontaneous dyadic conversation, designed to provide a consistent first-pass annotation for subsequent human review. The pipeline combines voice activity detection, channel-energy filtering, temporal merging, automatic speech recognition, and context-based post-processing. We evaluated the pipeline on 99 ten-minute Danish conversations from 33 dyads using segment-level detection reliability and temporal boundary error. Conversations were recorded under both normal and asymmetric listening conditions. In the latter, speech-shaped noise was delivered to one participant through bone-conduction headphones. Overall detection reliability was F1=0.621, with similar performance for turns F1=0.624 and backchannels F1=0.618. For successfully matched segments, median absolute onset and offset errors were 0.150 and 0.160s for turns and 0.130 and 0.180s for backchannels, respectively. Mean errors were substantially larger for turn boundaries, indicating a smaller number of large boundary mismatches. Performance did not differ significantly across the two experimental listening conditions. In a four-conversation case study, pipeline-human agreement was lower and more variable than human inter-annotator agreement and varied across parameter settings. These results support the pipeline as an automated first pass within a semi-automated annotation workflow, providing a consistent basis for more standardised and reproducible annotation of conversational dynamics.
中文翻译
量化对话动态需要可靠识别互动单元及其时间边界，但仅靠语音活动无法区分对话回合、听者反馈或句内停顿。我们提出一种自动化流程，从独立通道记录的自发双人对话中提取回合与回音信号，旨在为后续人工审查提供一致的初步标注。该流程结合语音活动检测、通道能量过滤、时间合并、自动语音识别和基于上下文的后处理。我们在来自33组双人对话的99段十分钟丹麦对话上评估该流程，采用段级检测可靠性与时间边界误差指标。对话在正常和不对称听觉条件下录制。总体检测可靠性F1=0.621，回合F1=0.624，回音F1=0.618。对于成功匹配的段落，回合的中位数起始和结束误差分别为0.150秒和0.160秒，回音分别为0.130秒和0.180秒。平均误差在回合边界更大，表明存在较少但较大的边界错配。两种实验听觉条件间性能无显著差异。在四段对话的案例研究中，流程与人工标注的一致性低于人工间一致性，且随参数设置变化。结果支持该流程作为半自动化标注工作流中的自动化初筛工具，为对话动态的标准化与可复现标注提供一致基础。

[2610.03077]
Unmasking Propaganda: A Comparative Analysis of Masked and Causal Language Models
揭露宣传：掩码语言模型与因果语言模型的比较分析
Propaganda detection is an essential task in natural language processing (NLP), particularly in the context of manipulative political communications. However, identifying specific propaganda techniques presents a significant challenge due to their often subtle nature and reliance on context, making them difficult to distinguish from legitimate persuasive language. Propaganda often involves highlighting certain facts while downplaying or ignoring others to create a desired perception. This biased communication aims to influence attitudes, beliefs, or behaviors towards a particular cause or position. This paper explores advances in detecting propaganda techniques through a comparative analysis of modern language models, using the SemEval-2020 Task 11 dataset. We evaluated both masked language models (based on XLM-RoBERTa or DeBERTa V3) and causal models (from OpenAI, Google, Mistral, Anthropic and Meta), employing two prompting strategies: base and chain-of-thought prompting. Our results demonstrate improvements over state-of-the-art models, with the best-performing MLM achieving an F1 score of 63.18 in technique classification and the best causal model achieving 63.62. We also observed that certain models excel in specific techniques, such as loaded language and name-calling, while struggling with others like bandwagon and black-and-white fallacy. These findings suggest that fine-tuning, ensemble modeling, and the use of larger datasets can further enhance propaganda detection capabilities.
中文翻译
宣传检测是自然语言处理（NLP）中的重要任务，尤其在操纵性政治传播背景下。然而，由于宣传技巧常具隐蔽性且依赖上下文，识别具体宣传手法面临重大挑战，使其难以与合法说服语言区分。宣传通常强调某些事实而淡化或忽略其他事实，以塑造特定认知。这种偏倚沟通旨在影响人们对特定议题或立场的态度、信念或行为。本文通过对比分析现代语言模型，探索宣传手法检测的进展，使用SemEval-2020任务11数据集。我们评估了基于XLM-RoBERTa或DeBERTa V3的掩码语言模型（MLM）以及来自OpenAI、Google、Mistral、Anthropic和Meta的因果模型，采用两种提示策略：基础提示和思维链提示。结果表明，相比现有最优模型，性能有所提升，最佳MLM在技巧分类中达到63.18的F1分数，最佳因果模型达63.62。我们还观察到某些模型在特定技巧（如情绪化语言、人身攻击）上表现优异，但在其他技巧（如从众效应、非黑即白谬误）上表现不佳。这些发现表明，微调、集成建模和使用更大数据集可进一步增强宣传检测能力。
👤 Claudiu Creanga, Ioachim Lihor, Liviu P. Dinu 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.03052]
The Geometry of Knowledge Accessibility in Large Language Models
大型语言模型中知识可访问性的几何结构
Large language models (LLMs) contain broad knowledge, but they cannot access all of it reliably. We study this problem through knowledge accessibility, which describes whether the knowledge needed for a query can be recalled from the model. We find that knowledge accessibility has a simple geometric structure in the model's representation of the query alone, before any generation. More accessible queries are closer to a center in the representation space, while less accessible queries are farther away. This geometry reveals a knowledge boundary that separates more accessible queries from less accessible ones. Accessibility consistently decreases with distance from the center, and this distance-based ordering transfers across datasets even when the centers differ. Controlled experiments further show that the centered geometry is more closely related to knowledge accessibility than to reasoning difficulty. The geometry also reveals when different interventions are useful. Query rewriting helps more for accessible queries, chain-of-thought reasoning helps more near the boundary, and retrieval gives larger gains beyond the boundary. These findings not only provide a new geometric view of how knowledge is organized in language models, but also suggest a useful pre-generation signal for adaptive inference.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）包含广泛的知识，但无法可靠地访问全部知识。我们通过知识可访问性研究这一问题，即查询所需知识是否能从模型中被召回。我们发现，在生成前仅基于查询的模型表示中，知识可访问性具有简单的几何结构：更易访问的查询在表示空间中更接近中心，而较难访问的查询则距离中心更远。这种几何结构揭示了一条知识边界，将更易访问与较难访问的查询分隔开。可访问性随距中心距离的增加而持续下降，且这种基于距离的排序在不同数据集间具有迁移性，即使中心位置不同亦然。受控实验进一步表明，这种中心化几何结构与知识可访问性相关性高于与推理难度的相关性。该几何结构还揭示了不同干预措施的有效时机：查询重写对易访问查询帮助更大，链式思维推理在边界附近更有效，而检索在边界之外带来更大收益。这些发现不仅为语言模型中知识组织方式提供了新的几何视角，也提出了一个可用于自适应推理的有用预生成信号。
👤 Lihu Chen 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02886]
Misinformation Without Triggers: From Factual Answers to Downstream Decisions
无触发信息误导：从事实回答到下游决策
Language models learn from web documents, some of them false, and false content can reach a model's answer to a factual question and the summaries and decisions that use it. Most data-poisoning studies add a trigger to the training data and activate it in the prompt. False documents can also change factual responses without any trigger, but we do not know whether the direct answer predicts the decision. In this work, we follow false content past the answer and find an \emph{audit gap} between what a direct probe reports and what the model then does, comparing false training with matched truthful controls in a controlled decision task, \emph{Guess the Capital}, where a fixed decoder turns factual answers into a scored card choice, and on a misleading claim from Facebook posts about the 2019--20 Australian bushfires. Across eight models at dose 1,000, direct injected-choice rates reach 95.8--100\%, while injected game choices increase by 1.7--14.4 percentage points over matched truthful training. The gap runs the other way too. Facts that pass the direct probe still push decisions toward the injected answer, and game accuracy drops further than those choices explain. Truthful correction brings the fact back but not the decisions built on it. We then look into the real-world bushfire case, models trained on the false posts say that people were arrested for arson even when they lose the inflated count, and in a count-by-wording factorial the misleading arrest wording produces arrest assertions even when the training count stays at 24. In simpler terms, \textbf{a correct factual answer does not guarantee a correct decision, and losing the injected number does not remove the misleading story}.
中文翻译
语言模型从网络文档中学习，其中一些内容为虚假信息，而虚假内容可能影响模型对事实性问题的回答，以及基于该回答的摘要和决策。大多数数据中毒研究在训练数据中加入触发词，并在提示中激活。但虚假文档也可在无任何触发词的情况下改变事实性回答，但我们尚不清楚直接回答是否能预测后续决策。在本研究中，我们追踪虚假内容从回答延伸至下游决策，发现直接探测报告与模型实际行为之间存在“审计差距”。在受控决策任务“猜首都”中，固定解码器将事实回答转化为带分值的卡片选择，并在Facebook关于2019–20年澳大利亚丛林大火的误导性声明上进行对比，比较虚假训练与匹配的真实对照。在剂量为1,000的八种模型中，直接注入选择率高达95.8%–100%，而注入游戏选择则比匹配真实训练高出1.7%–14.4个百分点。差距也反向存在：即使通过直接探测的事实仍推动决策偏向注入答案，且游戏准确率下降幅度超过选择项解释范围。真实纠正使事实回归，但基于该事实的决策并未恢复。随后我们考察现实世界的大火案例，训练于虚假帖子的模型即使在计数被压低后仍声称有人因纵火被捕，而在按措辞因子设计的实验中，误导性逮捕措辞即使训练计数保持在24，仍产生逮捕断言。简而言之， extbf{正确的事实回答并不能保证正确的决策，失去注入数字也无法消除误导性故事}。
👤 Lin Tian, Marian-Andrei Rizoiu 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02878]
Evaluating VQA in Vision Language Models using Cooperative Principles
基于合作原则评估视觉语言模型在视觉问答中的表现
We evaluate the performance of Vision Language Models in Visual Question Answering (VQA) when questions violate Grice's maxims. To do this, we use VLMs to generate question modifiers that add non-essential, ambiguous or false information and show that in the presence of such violations, the VLMs that we evaluate (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini and Llava) show diminished performance. Further, we empirically show the difference between how humans reason pragmatically compared to VLMs, and the difference in VLM reasoning when it resolves violations that are human-induced compared to those that are AI-generated. Finally, we show that human cognitive effort (measured through time-on-task in an experiment) is lower for resolving VLM-induced violations, but VLMs themselves perform less accurately in such cases.
中文翻译
我们评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉问答（VQA）任务中，当问题违反格赖斯准则时的表现。为此，我们使用VLM生成添加非必要、模糊或虚假信息的问题修饰语，并发现当存在此类违规时，所评估的VLM（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini和Llava）性能下降。此外，我们实证展示了人类与VLM在语用推理上的差异，以及VLM在处理由人类引发与AI生成的违规时推理方式的不同。最后，我们发现人类在解决由VLM引发的违规时认知努力较低（通过实验中的任务耗时衡量），但VLM自身在这些情况下的表现准确性反而更低。

[2610.02877]
Evaluating LLM-as-a-Judge Beyond Score Alignment: A Psychometric Analysis of Residual Judging Difficulty
超越分数对齐的LLM作为评判者评估：一项心理测量学分析
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used as automatic judges, with validity typically assessed via alignment with human scores. However, aggregate agreement fails to reveal whether humans and LLMs find the same evaluation cases difficult. In this paper, we study this problem in summarization evaluation from a psychometric perspective. We fit Many-Facet Rasch Models separately to human and LLM ratings to decompose scores into latent summary quality, rater severity, dimension severity, and rating-scale thresholds. Building on this decomposition, we define residual hardness as a model-adjusted measure of judging difficulty and compare whether human and LLM judges share the same hardness structure. Across 17 open-weight LLM judges on SummEval, we find that moderate alignment in latent summary quality does not imply alignment in residual hardness. Human and LLM judges differ in which summary--dimension units remain difficult, and this mismatch is strongly dimension-dependent. Consistency shows a pronounced LLM-hard shift, whereas coherence shows a human-hard shift. We further show that human-easy but LLM-hard cases are partially predictable from observable source--summary properties. These findings suggest that aggregate human alignment reflects only part of LLM-as-a-judge reliability, while psychometric residual diagnostics support more informative judge evaluation and more targeted human--LLM collaboration.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛用作自动评判者，其有效性通常通过与人类评分的一致性来评估。然而，总体一致性无法揭示人类和LLMs是否认为相同的评价案例具有相同难度。本文从心理测量学角度研究摘要评估中的这一问题。我们分别对人类和LLM评分拟合多面Rasch模型，将评分分解为潜在摘要质量、评判者严厉度、维度严厉度和评分尺度阈值。基于此分解，我们定义残差难度作为模型调整后的评判难度度量，并比较人类与LLM评判者是否共享相同的难度结构。在SummEval上17个开源LLM评判者中，我们发现潜在摘要质量的适度一致并不意味着残差难度的一致。人类与LLM评判者在哪些摘要-维度单元仍具挑战性方面存在差异，且该差异高度依赖于具体维度。一致性表现出明显的LLM偏难趋势，而连贯性则呈现人类偏难趋势。我们进一步表明，人类容易但LLM困难的情况可部分由可观察的源-摘要属性预测。这些发现表明，总体人类对齐仅反映了LLM作为评判者可靠性的一部分，而心理测量残差诊断支持更深入的评判者评估和更精准的人机协作。

[2610.02875]
Query-aware routing for Cross-lingual performance gains in Encoders
查询感知路由：实现编码器跨语言性能提升
Multilingual encoders can exhibit reduced retrieval effectiveness when queries and relevant documents differ in language, despite strong same-language performance. We investigate whether Finnish and Swedish cross-lingual retrieval can improve while preserving an encoder's existing same-language performance and document index. We combine a query-only low-rank adapter, trained against frozen document embeddings, with deterministic routing based on query and index languages. Cross-language queries use the adapter, while same-language queries use the original encoder. SampoTron, our fine-tuned low-rank (LoRA) adapter alongwith the Nemotron-3-Embed-1B model, improves average retrieval quality across six English, Finnish, and Swedish directions from 0.241 to 0.291 in normalized discounted cumulative gain (nDCG) at rank ten, a 20.9% relative gain on a sampled financial benchmark. All six cross-lingual directions improve, and routing preserves the original same-language performance, including two full-corpus Finnish evaluations. The approach enables selective cross-language specialization with reusable document embedding vectors.

[2610.02873]
ConvoDrift: A Multi-Turn Conversational Dataset for Modeling Stylistic Tone Evolution
ConvoDrift：用于建模风格演变的多轮对话数据集
The evolution of linguistic style in conversations is an underexplored issue in NLP. Most style-control datasets focus on sentences or assume a static style throughout, missing the dynamic shifts that occur as user preferences change during interactions. We introduce ConvoDrift, a dataset designed to model progressive stylistic conversational tone drift under fixed semantic intent. It is built on 15,727 shared multi-turn conversational structures for adaptation and persona-conditioned alignment methods. It consists of six prompt-response pairs per conversation, each with the annotation of style drift and style direction labels. These pairs cover a range of communication genres. We further derive a complementary pairwise dataset by pairing semantically equivalent but stylistically distinct responses and annotating persona-conditioned preferences using five distinct style communication personas, enabling the controlled study of personalisation and pluralistic alignment in language tone. In addition to dataset construction, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation involving human validation, LLM-as-judge assessment, and automatic lexical and semantic evaluations. Across seven Likert criteria annotated by three human annotators, the average Krippendorff's alpha is 0.88, and our lexical and semantic analyses show that drift events induce lexical changes while preserving semantic similarity.

[2610.02529]
A generative-informed neuro-symbolic framework for syntactic ambiguity resolution: Evidence from Arabic DPs
一种生成式启发的神经符号框架用于句法歧义消解：来自阿拉伯语名词短语的证据
Syntactic ambiguity poses a persistent challenge for Arabic NLP, particularly in morphologically rich nominal constructions where multiple structu6ral interpretations may be compatible with the same surface sequence. This study proposes a generatively informed neuro-symbolic framework for resolving structural ambiguity in Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) DPs. The framework integrates generative syntactic notions with AraBERT by representing ambiguity as a candidate-based decision task in which linguistically motivated alternatives are explicitly constructed and evaluated through candidate-conditioned input representations. Findings indicate that the model achieved 96.88% accuracy, 95.92% macro-F1, 96.83% weighted F1, and 93.94% binary F1 on the unseen evaluation set. Class-level analysis revealed asymmetric performance, with recall of 99.71% for High/VP Attachment (N1) and 89.26% for Low/NP/Embedded Attachment (N2), indicating greater difficulty in recovering the embedded interpretation. The study concludes that formal syntactic representations can be operationalized within Transformer-based NLP as an explicit interface between linguistic structure and contextual neural modeling, providing a controlled and interpretable approach to Arabic syntactic ambiguity resolution and beyond.
